};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicRecombinationClaimedComplete"->False|>;

persistenceMarkers=<|
"weightedBBNCarryForwardMarker"->N[Mean[{step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step4["w2rInternalCloseoutScore"],thermal["weightedBBNReadinessMarker"]}]],
"weightedLightElementMemoryMarker"->N[Mean[{step4["li7ProxyClosenessScore"],step4["lightElementDriftDisciplineScore"],step4["speciesCompletenessScore"]}]],
"weightedPhotonBathPendingMarker"->thermal["weightedPhotonBathContinuityMarker"],
"weightedEntropyPersistenceMarker"->thermal["weightedEntropyTransferMarker"],
"weightedBaryonPersistenceMarker"->seed["weightedBaryonPersistence"],
"weightedChargePersistenceMarker"->seed["chargeNeutralityProxy"],
"weightedPostBBNStateMarker"->N[Mean[{step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],seed["weightedBaryonPersistence"],seed["chargeNeutralityProxy"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"]}]]
|>;

bridgeScores=<|
"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->N[Mean[{step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step4["w2rInternalCloseoutScore"],
step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step3["weightedBBNReadinessScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"lightElementCarryScore"->N[Mean[{step4["li7ProxyClosenessScore"],step4["lightElementDriftDisciplineScore"],step4["speciesCompletenessScore"]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],seed["weightedBaryonPersistence"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["publicRecombinationClaimedComplete"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

bridgeState=<|
"adapterStatus"->"internal_BBN_to_recombination_persistence_bridge",
"recombinationPhysics"->"pending_not_solved",
"CMBSurface"->"pending_not_generated",
"photonBathNormalization"->"pending_not_invented",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep6W"->"ready_for_recombination_surface_module_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoPublicRecombinationClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicRecombinationClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"STEP4BBNPersistenceCarried"->(step4["bbnPersistenceScore"]>=thr),
"W2RInternalCloseoutCarried"->(step4["w2rInternalCloseoutScore"]>=thr),
"PersistenceMarkersGenerated"->(Length[persistenceMarkers]>=7),
"PendingRecombinationMarked"->StringContainsQ[bridgeState["recombinationPhysics"],"pending"],
"CMBSurfaceMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[bridgeState["CMBSurface"],"pending"],
"PhotonBathNormalizationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[bridgeState["photonBathNormalization"],"pending"],
"BBNToRecombinationPersistenceAboveThreshold"->(bridgeScores["bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"]>=thr),
"LightElementCarryAboveThreshold"->(bridgeScores["lightElementCarryScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(bridgeScores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(bridgeScores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(bridgeScores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
sci[x_]:=ToString[CForm[N[x]]];

step5=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->0.997764,

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

carried=<|
"eta10Proxy"->6.068,
"etaBFromEta10"->6.068*10^-10,
"chargeNeutralityProxy"->1,
"weightedBaryonPersistence"->0.994915,
"weightedHadronSubstrate"->470.833070,

|>;

"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,

"weightedBaryonPhotonMemoryMarker"->N[Mean[{carried["weightedBaryonPersistence"],carried["weightedPhotonBathPendingMarker"],carried["weightedEntropyPersistenceMarker"]}]],
"weightedChargeNeutralSurfaceMarker"->N[Mean[{carried["chargeNeutralityProxy"],carried["weightedChargePersistenceMarker"],step5["stateContinuityScore"]}]],
"weightedLightElementSurfaceCarryMarker"->N[Mean[{step5["lightElementCarryScore"],carried["weightedLightElementMemoryMarker"],step4["w2rInternalCloseoutScore"]}]],
"weightedCMBSeedContinuityMarker"->N[Mean[{kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"],carried["weightedPhotonBathPendingMarker"],carried["weightedEntropyPersistenceMarker"]}]],
"weightedVisibilityPendingMarker"->N[kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]],
"weightedRecombinationBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{weights["QV"],weights["RFL"],kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]}]]
|>;

"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_recombination_surface_readiness",

"visibilityFunction"->"pending_not_generated",
"CMBSurface"->"internal_seed_only_public_surface_pending",

|>;

scores=<|

surfaceMarkers["weightedLightElementSurfaceCarryMarker"],step5["bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"]}]],
"cmbSurfaceSeedDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step5["pendingDiscipline"],step5["notApplicableDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["physicalRecombinationConstantInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["publicCMBClaimedComplete"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalRecombinationConstantInvented"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoPhysicalRecombinationConstantInvented"->Not[viol["physicalRecombinationConstantInvented"]],
"NoPublicCMBClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicCMBClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"SurfaceMarkersGenerated"->(Length[surfaceMarkers]>=7),
"RecombinationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[surfaceState["physicalRecombinationRedshift"],"pending"],
"VisibilityFunctionMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[surfaceState["visibilityFunction"],"pending"],
"CMBTemperatureMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[surfaceState["CMBTemperatureNormalization"],"pending"],
"RecombinationReadinessAboveThreshold"->(scores["recombinationReadinessScore"]>=thr),
"CMBSurfaceSeedDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["cmbSurfaceSeedDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
sci[x_]:=ToString[CForm[N[x]]];

run="STEP 7W"; mod="Boltzmann / Transfer-Function Bridge"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP7W-v1.0"; thr=.95;

step0=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step1=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step2=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedSeedIntegrity"->0.998729|>;
step3=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedHandoffContinuityScore"->0.997030,"weightedBBNReadinessScore"->0.998642|>;
step4=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnPersistenceScore"->0.997207,"w2rInternalCloseoutScore"->0.997211|>;
step5=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->0.997764,"lightElementCarryScore"->0.997211,"stateContinuityScore"->0.996970|>;
step6=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"recombinationReadinessScore"->0.997826,
"cmbSurfaceSeedDisciplineScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484,
"pendingDiscipline"->1.000000,"notApplicableDiscipline"->1.000000|>;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

carried=<|
"eta10Proxy"->6.068,
"chargeNeutralityProxy"->1,
"weightedBaryonPersistence"->0.994915,
"weightedHadronSubstrate"->470.833070,
"weightedBBNCarryForwardMarker"->0.997173,
"weightedLightElementMemoryMarker"->0.997211,
"weightedPhotonBathPendingMarker"->0.034357,
"weightedEntropyPersistenceMarker"->0.416107,
"weightedPostBBNStateMarker"->0.997288,
"weightedRecombinationReadinessMarker"->0.997341,
"weightedBaryonPhotonMemoryMarker"->0.481793,
"weightedChargeNeutralSurfaceMarker"->0.998990,
"weightedLightElementSurfaceCarryMarker"->0.997211,
"weightedCMBSeedContinuityMarker"->0.161607,
"weightedVisibilityPendingMarker"->0.034357,
"weightedRecombinationBoundaryMarker"->0.343091
|>;

pending={
"physical Boltzmann hierarchy",
"public Boltzmann-code transfer functions",
"CMB TT/TE/EE spectra",
"CMB lensing potential spectrum",
"matter transfer function T(k)",
"sound horizon",
"angular diameter distance",
"optical depth",
"visibility function",
"reionization history",
"public CMB likelihood comparison",
"public matter-power likelihood comparison"
};

availableNotApplicable={
"W3 grouped nucleosynthesis cascade",
"W4 element-resolved abundance screen",
"CR-Trace global score",
"BAO/SNe comparison values",
"galaxy formation outputs",
"stellar nucleosynthesis outputs",
"public Planck/ACT/SPT comparison values",
"public DESI/SDSS/eBOSS comparison values"
};

forbidden={
"deleted prior notebook",
"older transfer logs",
"math buckets",
"prior preprints unless explicitly authorized",
"excluded simulation attempts",
"new public data during generation",
"scalar CIF/QV/RFL field representation",
"post-hoc transfer-function normalization",
"public Boltzmann-code values treated as generated"
};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicBoltzmannClaimedComplete"->False,
"physicalTransferFunctionInvented"->False|>;

transferMarkers=<|
"weightedRadiationMemoryMarker"->N[Mean[{carried["weightedPhotonBathPendingMarker"],carried["weightedEntropyPersistenceMarker"],kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]}]],
"weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"->N[Mean[{carried["weightedBaryonPersistence"],carried["weightedBaryonPhotonMemoryMarker"],carried["weightedRecombinationBoundaryMarker"]}]],
"weightedCMBTransferSeedMarker"->N[Mean[{carried["weightedCMBSeedContinuityMarker"],carried["weightedVisibilityPendingMarker"],kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]}]],
"weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"->N[Mean[{weights["QV"],weights["RFL"],carried["weightedPostBBNStateMarker"]}]],
"weightedChargeNeutralTransferMarker"->N[Mean[{carried["chargeNeutralityProxy"],carried["weightedChargeNeutralSurfaceMarker"],step6["stateContinuityScore"]}]],
"weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"->N[Mean[{carried["weightedLightElementMemoryMarker"],carried["weightedLightElementSurfaceCarryMarker"],step5["lightElementCarryScore"]}]],
"weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{step6["recombinationReadinessScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"]}]]
|>;

transferState=<|
"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_transfer_bridge_readiness",
"boltzmannHierarchy"->"pending_not_solved",
"transferFunctions"->"pending_not_generated",
"cmbSpectra"->"pending_not_generated",
"matterPowerSpectrum"->"pending_not_generated",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep8W"->"ready_for_expansion_distance_or_primordial_bridge_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

scores=<|
"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->N[Mean[{transferMarkers["weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"],step6["recombinationReadinessScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"]}]],
"cmbSeedDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step6["cmbSurfaceSeedDisciplineScore"],step6["pendingDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["publicBoltzmannClaimedComplete"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["physicalTransferFunctionInvented"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalTransferFunctionInvented"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP6WLocked"->(step6["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoPhysicalTransferFunctionInvented"->Not[viol["physicalTransferFunctionInvented"]],
"NoPublicBoltzmannClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicBoltzmannClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"TransferMarkersGenerated"->(Length[transferMarkers]>=7),
"BoltzmannHierarchyMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[transferState["boltzmannHierarchy"],"pending"],
"TransferFunctionsMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[transferState["transferFunctions"],"pending"],
"CMBSpectraMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[transferState["cmbSpectra"],"pending"],
"MatterPowerMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[transferState["matterPowerSpectrum"],"pending"],
"TransferBridgeReadinessAboveThreshold"->(scores["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]>=thr),
"CMBSeedDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["cmbSeedDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP6WLocked"]],Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];

cert=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 7W CERTIFICATE",
"================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE-CONTRIBUTION AUDIT",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["Available but not applicable values","IDENTIFIED_AND_EXCLUDED"],
ln["Missing / pending values","IDENTIFIED_NOT_INVENTED"],

},
Map["- "<>#&,forbidden],
{

ln["cmbSurfaceSeedDisciplineScore",num[step6["cmbSurfaceSeedDisciplineScore"]]],

},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,transferMarkers],
{

ln["transferBridgeReadinessScore",num[scores["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["cmbSeedDisciplineScore",num[scores["cmbSeedDisciplineScore"]]],

ln["transferFunctions",transferState["transferFunctions"]],

},

},

{

"NO-RETUNE / NO-FIT FIREWALL",
ln["Deleted prior notebook used",yn[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]]],

},

ln["Sub-0.95 rule",If[score<thr,"FAIL / ANALYZE","CLEAR"]],

}
];

run="STEP 8W"; mod="Expansion / Distance / BAO-SNe Bridge"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP8W-v1.0"; thr=.95;

step0=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step1=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step2=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedSeedIntegrity"->0.998729|>;
step3=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedHandoffContinuityScore"->0.997030,"weightedBBNReadinessScore"->0.998642|>;
step4=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnPersistenceScore"->0.997207,"w2rInternalCloseoutScore"->0.997211|>;
step5=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->0.997764,"stateContinuityScore"->0.996970|>;
step6=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"recombinationReadinessScore"->0.997826,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step7=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->0.997691,
"cmbSeedDisciplineScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484,
"pendingDiscipline"->1.000000,"notApplicableDiscipline"->1.000000|>;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

carried=<|
"weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"->0.997691,
"weightedRadiationMemoryMarker"->0.161607,
"weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"->0.606600,
"weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"->0.664068,
"weightedChargeNeutralTransferMarker"->0.998825,
"weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"->0.997211
|>;

expansionSource=<|
"H0Proxy"->70.173,
"earlyDarkMatterCompression"->0.216534,
"lateDarkMatterCompression"->0.0381948,
"darkMatterCompressionDecayRatio"->0.176392,
"earlyDarkEnergyTail"->2.52655,
"lateDarkEnergyTail"->2.48382,
"darkEnergyTailRatio"->0.983088,
"earlyHubbleProxy"->1.65623,
"lateHubbleProxy"->1.58809,
"hubbleProxyRatio"->0.958858
|>;

pending={
"physical H(z) solution",
"absolute luminosity distance ladder",
"angular diameter distance",
"BAO sound horizon",
"BAO likelihood",
"SNe distance-modulus likelihood",
"public DESI/SDSS/eBOSS comparison",
"public Pantheon/Pantheon+ comparison",
"public H0/Riess/SH0ES comparison",
"covariance matrix",
"posterior likelihood or fitted parameters"
};

availableNotApplicable={
"primordial perturbation spectrum",
"matter power spectrum",
"CMB TT/TE/EE spectra",
"CMB lensing potential",
"W3 grouped nucleosynthesis cascade",
"W4 element-resolved abundance screen",
"CR-Trace global score",
"galaxy formation outputs",
"stellar nucleosynthesis outputs"
};

forbidden={
"deleted prior notebook",
"older transfer logs",
"math buckets",
"prior preprints unless explicitly authorized",
"excluded simulation attempts",
"new public data during generation",
"scalar CIF/QV/RFL field representation",
"post-hoc H0 normalization",
"BAO/SNe public data treated as generated",
"distance-ladder fit or parameter search"
};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicDistanceClaimedComplete"->False,
"physicalDistanceFunctionInvented"->False|>;

expansionMarkers=<|
"weightedExpansionMemoryMarker"->N[Mean[{step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"],step6["recombinationReadinessScore"],step5["bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"]}]],
"weightedHubblePersistenceMarker"->expansionSource["hubbleProxyRatio"],
"weightedDarkEnergyTailPersistenceMarker"->expansionSource["darkEnergyTailRatio"],
"weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker"->expansionSource["darkMatterCompressionDecayRatio"],
"weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker"->N[Mean[{expansionSource["hubbleProxyRatio"],expansionSource["darkEnergyTailRatio"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]}]],
"weightedBAOSNeBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{step7["cmbSeedDisciplineScore"],step7["pendingDiscipline"],step7["notApplicableDiscipline"]}]],
"weightedExpansionBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{weights["QV"],decomp["darkKernelBridge"],kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"],expansionSource["hubbleProxyRatio"]}]]
|>;

expansionState=<|
"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_expansion_distance_bridge_readiness",
"physicalHubbleFunction"->"pending_not_solved",
"distanceLadder"->"pending_not_generated",
"BAOLikelihood"->"pending_not_generated",
"SNeLikelihood"->"pending_not_generated",
"soundHorizon"->"pending_not_invented",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep9W"->"ready_for_primordial_or_growth_bridge_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

scores=<|
"expansionPersistenceScore"->N[Mean[{expansionMarkers["weightedExpansionMemoryMarker"],expansionMarkers["weightedHubblePersistenceMarker"],expansionMarkers["weightedDarkEnergyTailPersistenceMarker"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]}]],
"distanceBridgeDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step7["pendingDiscipline"],step7["notApplicableDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["physicalDistanceFunctionInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["publicDistanceClaimedComplete"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step7["stateContinuityScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalDistanceFunctionInvented"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP6WLocked"->(step6["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP7WLocked"->(step7["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoPhysicalDistanceFunctionInvented"->Not[viol["physicalDistanceFunctionInvented"]],
"NoPublicDistanceClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicDistanceClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"ExpansionMarkersGenerated"->(Length[expansionMarkers]>=7),
"HubbleFunctionMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[expansionState["physicalHubbleFunction"],"pending"],
"DistanceLadderMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[expansionState["distanceLadder"],"pending"],
"BAOLikelihoodMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[expansionState["BAOLikelihood"],"pending"],
"SNeLikelihoodMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[expansionState["SNeLikelihood"],"pending"],
"ExpansionPersistenceAboveThreshold"->(scores["expansionPersistenceScore"]>=thr),
"DistanceBridgeDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["distanceBridgeDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP6WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP7WLocked"]],
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];

},

run="STEP 9W"; mod="Primordial Perturbation / Matter Growth Bridge"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP9W-v1.0"; thr=.95;

step0=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step1=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step2=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedSeedIntegrity"->0.998729|>;
step3=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedHandoffContinuityScore"->0.997030,"weightedBBNReadinessScore"->0.998642|>;
step4=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnPersistenceScore"->0.997207,"w2rInternalCloseoutScore"->0.997211|>;
step5=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->0.997764,"stateContinuityScore"->0.996970|>;
step6=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"recombinationReadinessScore"->0.997826,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step7=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->0.997691,
"cmbSeedDisciplineScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step8=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349,
"distanceBridgeDisciplineScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484,
"pendingDiscipline"->1.000000,"notApplicableDiscipline"->1.000000|>;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

transferCarry=<|
"weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"->0.997691,
"weightedRadiationMemoryMarker"->0.161607,
"weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"->0.606600,
"weightedCMBTransferSeedMarker"->0.076774,
"weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"->0.664068,
"weightedChargeNeutralTransferMarker"->0.998825,
"weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"->0.997211
|>;

expansionCarry=<|
"H0Proxy"->70.173,
"darkMatterCompressionDecayRatio"->0.176392,
"darkEnergyTailRatio"->0.983088,
"hubbleProxyRatio"->0.958858,
"weightedExpansionMemoryMarker"->0.997760,
"weightedHubblePersistenceMarker"->0.958858,
"weightedDarkEnergyTailPersistenceMarker"->0.983088,
"weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker"->0.979879,
"weightedBAOSNeBoundaryMarker"->1.000000
|>;

growthGeometrySource=<|
"sigma8ProxyInternal"->0.808,
"S8ProxyInternal"->0.808,
"normalizedPsiRMS"->1.000000,
"fractalCoherenceProxy"->0.812251,
"geometryCoherenceMean"->0.940535,
"spinFoamWeightInitial"->0.875122,
"spinFoamWeightFinal"->0.976745
|>;

pending={
"physical primordial scalar spectrum P_R(k)",
"scalar spectral index n_s",
"amplitude A_s",
"running alpha_s",
"tensor-to-scalar ratio r",
"physical matter transfer function T(k)",
"linear matter power spectrum P(k)",
"nonlinear matter power spectrum",
"sigma8 public likelihood",
"S8 public likelihood",
"growth rate fσ8 likelihood",
"halo mass function",
"public CMB/matter-power likelihood comparison",
"covariance matrix",
"posterior likelihood or fitted parameters"
};

availableNotApplicable={
"W3 grouped nucleosynthesis cascade",
"W4 element-resolved abundance screen",
"CR-Trace global score",
"BAO/SNe public comparison values",
"galaxy formation outputs",
"stellar nucleosynthesis outputs",
"survey window functions",
"mock catalog generation",
"weak lensing shear maps"
};

forbidden={
"deleted prior notebook",
"older transfer logs",
"math buckets",
"prior preprints unless explicitly authorized",
"excluded simulation attempts",
"new public data during generation",
"scalar CIF/QV/RFL field representation",
"post-hoc sigma8/S8 normalization",
"matter-power public data treated as generated",
"primordial spectrum fit or parameter search"
};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicGrowthClaimedComplete"->False,
"physicalPowerSpectrumInvented"->False|>;

growthMarkers=<|
"weightedPrimordialSeedMarker"->N[Mean[{transferCarry["weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"],expansionCarry["weightedExpansionMemoryMarker"],kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]}]],
"weightedMatterCouplingMarker"->transferCarry["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"],
"weightedRadiationMatterMemoryMarker"->N[Mean[{transferCarry["weightedRadiationMemoryMarker"],transferCarry["weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"],transferCarry["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"]}]],
"weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier"->N[Mean[{step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],expansionCarry["weightedHubblePersistenceMarker"],expansionCarry["weightedDarkEnergyTailPersistenceMarker"]}]],
"weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker"->growthGeometrySource["geometryCoherenceMean"],
"weightedFractalCoherenceMarker"->growthGeometrySource["fractalCoherenceProxy"],
"weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker"->growthGeometrySource["sigma8ProxyInternal"],
"weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step8["stateContinuityScore"]}]]
|>;

growthState=<|
"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_primordial_growth_bridge_readiness",
"primordialSpectrum"->"pending_not_generated",
"matterTransferFunction"->"pending_not_generated",
"matterPowerSpectrum"->"pending_not_generated",
"sigma8Likelihood"->"pending_not_generated",
"S8Likelihood"->"pending_not_generated",
"growthRateLikelihood"->"pending_not_generated",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep10W"->"ready_for_halo_galaxy_or_growth_extension_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

scores=<|
"primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"->N[Mean[{growthMarkers["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step8["stateContinuityScore"]}]],
"growthDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step8["pendingDiscipline"],step8["notApplicableDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["physicalPowerSpectrumInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["publicGrowthClaimedComplete"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step8["stateContinuityScore"],step7["stateContinuityScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalPowerSpectrumInvented"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP6WLocked"->(step6["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP7WLocked"->(step7["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP8WLocked"->(step8["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoPhysicalPowerSpectrumInvented"->Not[viol["physicalPowerSpectrumInvented"]],
"NoPublicGrowthClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicGrowthClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"GrowthMarkersGenerated"->(Length[growthMarkers]>=8),
"PrimordialSpectrumMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[growthState["primordialSpectrum"],"pending"],
"MatterTransferMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[growthState["matterTransferFunction"],"pending"],
"MatterPowerMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[growthState["matterPowerSpectrum"],"pending"],
"Sigma8LikelihoodMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[growthState["sigma8Likelihood"],"pending"],
"S8LikelihoodMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[growthState["S8Likelihood"],"pending"],
"PrimordialGrowthBridgeReadinessAboveThreshold"->(scores["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"]>=thr),
"GrowthDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["growthDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP6WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP7WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP8WLocked"]],
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];

cert=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 9W CERTIFICATE",
"================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE-CONTRIBUTION AUDIT",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["Available but not applicable values","IDENTIFIED_AND_EXCLUDED"],
ln["Missing / pending values","IDENTIFIED_NOT_INVENTED"],
ln["Forbidden / noncanonical values","EXCLUDED"],
ln["Values not generated by this step","IDENTIFIED"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"AUTHORIZED INPUTS",
"- STEP 0W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 1W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 2W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 3W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 4W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 5W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 6W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 7W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 8W PASS_LOCKED",
"- Recursive Fractal Cosmology: The Triadic Emergence of Existence - A Triadic Theorem - Revised W2-R/W3/W4/CR-Trace public theorem metadata appendix",
"- RFC SU v1 Reproducibility Metadata and Wolfram Ledger, Section 1",
"- RFC-SU RFC SU v1 Architecture Ledger latest",
"",
"FORBIDDEN INPUTS / METHODS"
},
Map["- "<>#&,forbidden],
{
"",
"FIRST ACTION",
ln["globalFirstAction",firstAction],
ln["weightedFirstAction",weightedFirstAction],
"",
"TRIAD-WEIGHT DECOMPOSITION",
ln["qvCore",num[decomp["qvCore"]]],
ln["darkKernelBridge",num[decomp["darkKernelBridge"]]],
ln["cifPower",num[decomp["cifPower"]]],
ln["rflPower",num[decomp["rflPower"]]],
ln["CIFWeight",num[weights["CIF"]]],
ln["QVWeight",num[weights["QV"]]],
ln["RFLWeight",num[weights["RFL"]]],
ln["weightSum",num[Total[Values[weights]]]],
"",
"STEP 7W / STEP 8W CARRIED STATE",
ln["transferBridgeReadinessScore",num[step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["expansionPersistenceScore",num[step8["expansionPersistenceScore"]]],
ln["distanceBridgeDisciplineScore",num[step8["distanceBridgeDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[step8["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["weightedTransferBoundaryMarker",num[transferCarry["weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"]]],
ln["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker",num[transferCarry["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"]]],
ln["weightedExpansionMemoryMarker",num[expansionCarry["weightedExpansionMemoryMarker"]]],
ln["weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker",num[expansionCarry["weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker"]]],
"",
"RFC INTERNAL GROWTH / GEOMETRY SOURCE INPUTS",
ln["sigma8ProxyInternal",num[growthGeometrySource["sigma8ProxyInternal"]]],
ln["S8ProxyInternal",num[growthGeometrySource["S8ProxyInternal"]]],
ln["normalizedPsiRMS",num[growthGeometrySource["normalizedPsiRMS"]]],
ln["fractalCoherenceProxy",num[growthGeometrySource["fractalCoherenceProxy"]]],
ln["geometryCoherenceMean",num[growthGeometrySource["geometryCoherenceMean"]]],
ln["spinFoamWeightInitial",num[growthGeometrySource["spinFoamWeightInitial"]]],
ln["spinFoamWeightFinal",num[growthGeometrySource["spinFoamWeightFinal"]]],
"",
"INTERNAL WEIGHTED PRIMORDIAL / GROWTH MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,growthMarkers],
{
"",
"PRIMORDIAL / MATTER-GROWTH BRIDGE SCORES",
ln["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore",num[scores["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["growthDisciplineScore",num[scores["growthDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[scores["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["sourceCarryCompleteness",num[scores["sourceCarryCompleteness"]]],
ln["pendingDiscipline",num[scores["pendingDiscipline"]]],
ln["notApplicableDiscipline",num[scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]]],
"",
"GROWTH STATE BOUNDARIES",
ln["moduleStatus",growthState["moduleStatus"]],
ln["primordialSpectrum",growthState["primordialSpectrum"]],
ln["matterTransferFunction",growthState["matterTransferFunction"]],
ln["matterPowerSpectrum",growthState["matterPowerSpectrum"]],
ln["sigma8Likelihood",growthState["sigma8Likelihood"]],
ln["S8Likelihood",growthState["S8Likelihood"]],
ln["growthRateLikelihood",growthState["growthRateLikelihood"]],
ln["publicComparison",growthState["publicComparison"]],
ln["handoffToStep10W",growthState["handoffToStep10W"]],
"",
"PENDING / NOT INVENTED VALUES"
},
Map["- "<>#&,pending],
{
"",
"AVAILABLE BUT NOT APPLICABLE TO STEP 9W"
},
Map["- "<>#&,availableNotApplicable],
{
"",
"NO-RETUNE / NO-FIT FIREWALL",
ln["Deleted prior notebook used",yn[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]]],
ln["Public data used during generation",yn[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]]],
ln["Parameter search performed",yn[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]]],
ln["Curve fitting performed",yn[viol["curveFitPerformed"]]],
ln["Post-hoc correction performed",yn[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]]],
ln["Upstream retune performed",yn[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]]],
ln["Scalar triad fields used",yn[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]]],
ln["Missing value invented",yn[viol["missingValueInvented"]]],
ln["Physical power spectrum invented",yn[viol["physicalPowerSpectrumInvented"]]],
ln["Public growth claimed complete",yn[viol["publicGrowthClaimedComplete"]]],
ln["Available-but-not-applicable value consumed",yn[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]]],
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",thr],
ln["STEP 9W score",num[score]],
ln["Sub-0.95 rule",If[score<thr,"FAIL / ANALYZE","CLEAR"]],
"",
"VIOLATION CHECK",
ln["Automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
"",
"HANDOFF PACKET",
ln["fromModule","STEP 9W Primordial Perturbation / Matter Growth Bridge"],
ln["toModule","STEP 10W Halo / Galaxy / Nonlinear Growth Bridge"],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUses","Initialize STEP 10W only if PASS_LOCKED"],
ln["forbiddenNextUses","Do not treat STEP 9W as physical primordial spectrum, public matter power, sigma8/S8 likelihood, or fitted growth model"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 9W derives internal weighted primordial/matter-growth bridge-readiness markers from locked RFC sources. It does not generate a physical primordial spectrum, matter transfer function, matter power spectrum, sigma8/S8 likelihood, growth-rate likelihood, public comparison, tuning, fitting, post-hoc correction, or scalar triad-field modeling.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[cert,"\n"]];

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 10W"; mod="Halo / Galaxy / Nonlinear Growth Bridge"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP10W-v1.0"; thr=.95;

step0=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step1=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step2=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedSeedIntegrity"->0.998729|>;
step3=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedHandoffContinuityScore"->0.997030|>;
step4=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnPersistenceScore"->0.997207|>;
step5=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->0.997764,"stateContinuityScore"->0.996970|>;
step6=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"recombinationReadinessScore"->0.997826,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step7=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->0.997691,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step8=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step9=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"->0.993175,
"growthDisciplineScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484,
"pendingDiscipline"->1.000000,"notApplicableDiscipline"->1.000000|>;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

growthCarry=<|
"weightedPrimordialSeedMarker"->0.676603,
"weightedMatterCouplingMarker"->0.664068,
"weightedRadiationMatterMemoryMarker"->0.477425,
"weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier"->0.975432,
"weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker"->0.940535,
"weightedFractalCoherenceMarker"->0.812251,
"weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker"->0.808000,
"weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"->0.993175,
"sigma8ProxyInternal"->0.808000,
"S8ProxyInternal"->0.808000,
"normalizedPsiRMS"->1.000000,
"fractalCoherenceProxy"->0.812251,
"geometryCoherenceMean"->0.940535,
"spinFoamWeightInitial"->0.875122,
"spinFoamWeightFinal"->0.976745
|>;

expansionCarry=<|
"weightedExpansionMemoryMarker"->0.997760,
"weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker"->0.176392,
"weightedDarkEnergyTailPersistenceMarker"->0.983088,
"weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker"->0.979879,
"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349
|>;

transferCarry=<|
"weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"->0.997691,
"weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"->0.664068,
"weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"->0.606600,
"weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"->0.997211,
"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->0.997691
|>;

pending={
"physical halo mass function",
"nonlinear matter power spectrum",
"halo concentration relation",
"halo merger tree",
"galaxy formation model",
"stellar-to-halo mass relation",
"baryonic feedback model",
"weak lensing shear field",
"mock galaxy catalog",
"survey selection function",
"public clustering likelihood",
"public weak-lensing likelihood",
"public halo/galaxy comparison",
"covariance matrix",
"posterior likelihood or fitted parameters"
};

availableNotApplicable={
"W3 grouped nucleosynthesis cascade",
"W4 element-resolved abundance screen",
"CR-Trace global score",
"stellar population synthesis",
"galactic chemical evolution",
"survey window functions",
"photometric projection",
"reionization / cosmic dawn module",
"public DES/KiDS/HSC/Euclid/Rubin comparison values"
};

forbidden={
"deleted prior notebook",
"older transfer logs",
"math buckets",
"prior preprints unless explicitly authorized",
"excluded simulation attempts",
"new public data during generation",
"scalar CIF/QV/RFL field representation",
"post-hoc halo normalization",
"public clustering/lensing data treated as generated",
"nonlinear spectrum fit or parameter search"
};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"->False,
"physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"->False,"nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"->False|>;

haloMarkers=<|
"weightedHaloSeedMarker"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"],growthCarry["weightedMatterCouplingMarker"],expansionCarry["weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier"]/.expansionCarry->growthCarry}]],
"weightedNonlinearMemoryMarker"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["weightedFractalCoherenceMarker"],growthCarry["weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker"],growthCarry["normalizedPsiRMS"]}]],
"weightedMatterCollapseCarrier"->N[Mean[{transferCarry["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"],expansionCarry["weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker"],growthCarry["weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker"]}]],
"weightedGeometryHaloCarrier"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["geometryCoherenceMean"],growthCarry["spinFoamWeightInitial"],growthCarry["spinFoamWeightFinal"]}]],
"weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"],step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]}]],
"weightedBaryonStructureCarryMarker"->N[Mean[{transferCarry["weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"],transferCarry["weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"],step5["bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"]}]],
"weightedStructureBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"],step6["recombinationReadinessScore"]}]]
|>;

haloState=<|
"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_halo_galaxy_nonlinear_bridge_readiness",
"haloMassFunction"->"pending_not_generated",
"nonlinearMatterPower"->"pending_not_generated",
"mergerTree"->"pending_not_generated",
"galaxyFormation"->"pending_not_generated",
"baryonicFeedback"->"pending_not_generated",
"mockCatalog"->"pending_not_generated",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep11W"->"ready_for_star_formation_or_galaxy_extension_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

scores=<|
"haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"->N[Mean[{haloMarkers["weightedStructureBoundaryMarker"],haloMarkers["weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"],step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]}]],
"nonlinearDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step9["pendingDiscipline"],step9["notApplicableDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["publicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step9["stateContinuityScore"],step8["stateContinuityScore"],step7["stateContinuityScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"]] && Not[viol["nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP6WLocked"->(step6["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP7WLocked"->(step7["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP8WLocked"->(step8["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP9WLocked"->(step9["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoHaloMassFunctionInvented"->Not[viol["physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"]],
"NoNonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"->Not[viol["nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"]],
"NoPublicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"HaloMarkersGenerated"->(Length[haloMarkers]>=7),
"HaloMassFunctionMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["haloMassFunction"],"pending"],
"NonlinearMatterPowerMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["nonlinearMatterPower"],"pending"],
"GalaxyFormationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["galaxyFormation"],"pending"],
"MockCatalogMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["mockCatalog"],"pending"],
"HaloGalaxyBridgeReadinessAboveThreshold"->(scores["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"]>=thr),
"NonlinearDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["nonlinearDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP6WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP7WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP8WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP9WLocked"]],Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];

cert=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 10W CERTIFICATE",
"=================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE-CONTRIBUTION AUDIT",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["Available but not applicable values","IDENTIFIED_AND_EXCLUDED"],
ln["Missing / pending values","IDENTIFIED_NOT_INVENTED"],
ln["Forbidden / noncanonical values","EXCLUDED"],
ln["Values not generated by this step","IDENTIFIED"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"AUTHORIZED INPUTS",
"- STEP 0W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 1W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 2W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 3W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 4W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 5W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 6W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 7W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 8W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 9W PASS_LOCKED",
"- Recursive Fractal Cosmology: The Triadic Emergence of Existence - A Triadic Theorem - Revised W2-R/W3/W4/CR-Trace public theorem metadata appendix",
"- RFC SU v1 Reproducibility Metadata and Wolfram Ledger, Section 1",
"- RFC-SU RFC SU v1 Architecture Ledger latest",
"",
"FORBIDDEN INPUTS / METHODS"
},
Map["- "<>#&,forbidden],
{
"",
"FIRST ACTION",
ln["globalFirstAction",firstAction],
ln["weightedFirstAction",weightedFirstAction],
"",
"TRIAD-WEIGHT DECOMPOSITION",
ln["qvCore",num[decomp["qvCore"]]],
ln["darkKernelBridge",num[decomp["darkKernelBridge"]]],
ln["cifPower",num[decomp["cifPower"]]],
ln["rflPower",num[decomp["rflPower"]]],
ln["CIFWeight",num[weights["CIF"]]],
ln["QVWeight",num[weights["QV"]]],
ln["RFLWeight",num[weights["RFL"]]],
ln["weightSum",num[Total[Values[weights]]]],
"",
"STEP 9W CARRIED GROWTH / GEOMETRY INPUTS",
ln["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore",num[step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["growthDisciplineScore",num[step9["growthDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[step9["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"]]],
ln["weightedMatterCouplingMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedMatterCouplingMarker"]]],
ln["weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker"]]],
ln["weightedFractalCoherenceMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedFractalCoherenceMarker"]]],
ln["weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker"]]],
ln["sigma8ProxyInternal",num[growthCarry["sigma8ProxyInternal"]]],
ln["S8ProxyInternal",num[growthCarry["S8ProxyInternal"]]],
"",
"STEP 8W / STEP 7W STRUCTURE-CARRY INPUTS",
ln["expansionPersistenceScore",num[step8["expansionPersistenceScore"]]],
ln["transferBridgeReadinessScore",num[step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker",num[transferCarry["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"]]],
ln["weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker",num[expansionCarry["weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker"]]],
ln["weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker",num[expansionCarry["weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker"]]],
"",
"INTERNAL WEIGHTED HALO / GALAXY / NONLINEAR MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,haloMarkers],
{
"",
"HALO / GALAXY / NONLINEAR BRIDGE SCORES",
ln["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore",num[scores["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["nonlinearDisciplineScore",num[scores["nonlinearDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[scores["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["sourceCarryCompleteness",num[scores["sourceCarryCompleteness"]]],
ln["pendingDiscipline",num[scores["pendingDiscipline"]]],
ln["notApplicableDiscipline",num[scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]]],
"",
"HALO / GALAXY STATE BOUNDARIES",
ln["moduleStatus",haloState["moduleStatus"]],
ln["haloMassFunction",haloState["haloMassFunction"]],
ln["nonlinearMatterPower",haloState["nonlinearMatterPower"]],
ln["mergerTree",haloState["mergerTree"]],
ln["galaxyFormation",haloState["galaxyFormation"]],
ln["baryonicFeedback",haloState["baryonicFeedback"]],
ln["mockCatalog",haloState["mockCatalog"]],
ln["publicComparison",haloState["publicComparison"]],
ln["handoffToStep11W",haloState["handoffToStep11W"]],
"",
"PENDING / NOT INVENTED VALUES"
},
Map["- "<>#&,pending],
{
"",
"AVAILABLE BUT NOT APPLICABLE TO STEP 10W"
},
Map["- "<>#&,availableNotApplicable],
{
"",
"NO-RETUNE / NO-FIT FIREWALL",
ln["Deleted prior notebook used",yn[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]]],
ln["Public data used during generation",yn[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]]],
ln["Parameter search performed",yn[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]]],
ln["Curve fitting performed",yn[viol["curveFitPerformed"]]],
ln["Post-hoc correction performed",yn[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]]],
ln["Upstream retune performed",yn[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]]],
ln["Scalar triad fields used",yn[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]]],
ln["Missing value invented",yn[viol["missingValueInvented"]]],
ln["Halo mass function invented",yn[viol["physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"]]],
ln["Nonlinear power spectrum invented",yn[viol["nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"]]],
ln["Public halo/galaxy claimed complete",yn[viol["publicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"]]],
ln["Available-but-not-applicable value consumed",yn[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]]],
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",thr],
ln["STEP 10W score",num[score]],
ln["Sub-0.95 rule",If[score<thr,"FAIL / ANALYZE","CLEAR"]],
"",
"VIOLATION CHECK",
ln["Automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
"",
"HANDOFF PACKET",
ln["fromModule","STEP 10W Halo / Galaxy / Nonlinear Growth Bridge"],
ln["toModule","STEP 11W Star Formation / IMF / W3 Adapter"],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUses","Initialize STEP 11W only if PASS_LOCKED"],
ln["forbiddenNextUses","Do not treat STEP 10W as halo mass function, galaxy formation model, nonlinear matter power, mock catalog, public clustering/lensing comparison, or fitted structure model"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 10W derives internal weighted halo/galaxy/nonlinear-growth bridge-readiness markers from locked RFC sources. It does not generate a physical halo mass function, nonlinear matter power spectrum, galaxy formation model, mock catalog, public clustering/lensing likelihood, tuning, fitting, post-hoc correction, or scalar triad-field modeling.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[cert,"\n"]];

HANDOFF PACKET
fromModule: STEP 10W Halo / Galaxy / Nonlinear Growth Bridge
toModule: STEP 11W Star Formation / IMF / W3 Adapter
outputLockStatus: PASS_LOCKED
allowedNextUses: Initialize STEP 11W only if PASS_LOCKED
forbiddenNextUses: Do not treat STEP 10W as halo mass function, galaxy formation model, nonlinear matter power, mock catalog, public clustering/lensing comparison, or fitted structure model

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 10W"; mod="Halo / Galaxy / Nonlinear Growth Bridge"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP10W-v1.1"; thr=.95;

step0=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step1=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step2=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedSeedIntegrity"->0.998729|>;
step3=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedHandoffContinuityScore"->0.997030|>;
step4=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnPersistenceScore"->0.997207|>;
step5=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->0.997764,"stateContinuityScore"->0.996970|>;
step6=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"recombinationReadinessScore"->0.997826,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step7=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->0.997691,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step8=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step9=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"->0.993175,
"growthDisciplineScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484,
"pendingDiscipline"->1.000000,"notApplicableDiscipline"->1.000000|>;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

growthCarry=<|
"weightedPrimordialSeedMarker"->0.676603,
"weightedMatterCouplingMarker"->0.664068,
"weightedRadiationMatterMemoryMarker"->0.477425,
"weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier"->0.975432,
"weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker"->0.940535,
"weightedFractalCoherenceMarker"->0.812251,
"weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker"->0.808000,
"weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"->0.993175,
"sigma8ProxyInternal"->0.808000,
"S8ProxyInternal"->0.808000,
"normalizedPsiRMS"->1.000000,
"fractalCoherenceProxy"->0.812251,
"geometryCoherenceMean"->0.940535,
"spinFoamWeightInitial"->0.875122,
"spinFoamWeightFinal"->0.976745
|>;

expansionCarry=<|
"weightedExpansionMemoryMarker"->0.997760,
"weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker"->0.176392,
"weightedDarkEnergyTailPersistenceMarker"->0.983088,
"weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker"->0.979879,
"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349
|>;

transferCarry=<|
"weightedTransferBoundaryMarker"->0.997691,
"weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"->0.664068,
"weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"->0.606600,
"weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"->0.997211,
"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->0.997691
|>;

pending={
"physical halo mass function","nonlinear matter power spectrum","halo concentration relation",
"halo merger tree","galaxy formation model","stellar-to-halo mass relation",
"baryonic feedback model","weak lensing shear field","mock galaxy catalog",
"survey selection function","public clustering likelihood","public weak-lensing likelihood",
"public halo/galaxy comparison","covariance matrix","posterior likelihood or fitted parameters"
};

availableNotApplicable={
"W3 grouped nucleosynthesis cascade","W4 element-resolved abundance screen","CR-Trace global score",
"stellar population synthesis","galactic chemical evolution","survey window functions",
"photometric projection","reionization / cosmic dawn module",
"public DES/KiDS/HSC/Euclid/Rubin comparison values"
};

forbidden={
"deleted prior notebook","older transfer logs","math buckets","prior preprints unless explicitly authorized",
"excluded simulation attempts","new public data during generation","scalar CIF/QV/RFL field representation",
"post-hoc halo normalization","public clustering/lensing data treated as generated",
"nonlinear spectrum fit or parameter search"
};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"->False,
"physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"->False,"nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"->False|>;

haloMarkers=<|
"weightedHaloSeedMarker"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"],growthCarry["weightedMatterCouplingMarker"],growthCarry["weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier"]}]],
"weightedNonlinearMemoryMarker"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["weightedFractalCoherenceMarker"],growthCarry["weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker"],growthCarry["normalizedPsiRMS"]}]],
"weightedMatterCollapseCarrier"->N[Mean[{transferCarry["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"],expansionCarry["weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker"],growthCarry["weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker"]}]],
"weightedGeometryHaloCarrier"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["geometryCoherenceMean"],growthCarry["spinFoamWeightInitial"],growthCarry["spinFoamWeightFinal"]}]],
"weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"->N[Mean[{growthCarry["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"],step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]}]],
"weightedBaryonStructureCarryMarker"->N[Mean[{transferCarry["weightedBaryonAcousticSeedMarker"],transferCarry["weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"],step5["bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"]}]],
"weightedStructureBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"],step6["recombinationReadinessScore"]}]]
|>;

haloMarkersNumeric=And@@(NumericQ /@ Values[haloMarkers]);
haloMarkersNoMissing=FreeQ[Values[haloMarkers],_Missing];

haloState=<|
"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_halo_galaxy_nonlinear_bridge_readiness",
"haloMassFunction"->"pending_not_generated",
"nonlinearMatterPower"->"pending_not_generated",
"mergerTree"->"pending_not_generated",
"galaxyFormation"->"pending_not_generated",
"baryonicFeedback"->"pending_not_generated",
"mockCatalog"->"pending_not_generated",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep11W"->"ready_for_star_formation_or_galaxy_extension_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

scores=<|
"haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"->N[Mean[{haloMarkers["weightedStructureBoundaryMarker"],haloMarkers["weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"],step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"],step8["expansionPersistenceScore"],step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]}]],
"nonlinearDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step9["pendingDiscipline"],step9["notApplicableDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["publicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step9["stateContinuityScore"],step8["stateContinuityScore"],step7["stateContinuityScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"]] && Not[viol["nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

scoresNumeric=And@@(NumericQ /@ Values[scores]);
scoresNoMissing=FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing];

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP6WLocked"->(step6["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP7WLocked"->(step7["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP8WLocked"->(step8["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP9WLocked"->(step9["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoHaloMassFunctionInvented"->Not[viol["physicalHaloMassFunctionInvented"]],
"NoNonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"->Not[viol["nonlinearPowerSpectrumInvented"]],
"NoPublicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicHaloGalaxyClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"HaloMarkersGenerated"->(Length[haloMarkers]>=7),
"HaloMarkersNumeric"->haloMarkersNumeric,
"HaloMarkersNoMissing"->haloMarkersNoMissing,
"ScoresNumeric"->scoresNumeric,
"ScoresNoMissing"->scoresNoMissing,
"HaloMassFunctionMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["haloMassFunction"],"pending"],
"NonlinearMatterPowerMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["nonlinearMatterPower"],"pending"],
"GalaxyFormationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["galaxyFormation"],"pending"],
"MockCatalogMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[haloState["mockCatalog"],"pending"],
"HaloGalaxyBridgeReadinessAboveThreshold"->(scores["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"]>=thr),
"NonlinearDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["nonlinearDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP6WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP7WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP8WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP9WLocked"]],Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],
Not[checks["HaloMarkersNumeric"]],Not[checks["HaloMarkersNoMissing"]],
Not[checks["ScoresNumeric"]],Not[checks["ScoresNoMissing"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];

cert=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 10W CERTIFICATE",
"=================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE-CONTRIBUTION AUDIT",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["Available but not applicable values","IDENTIFIED_AND_EXCLUDED"],
ln["Missing / pending values","IDENTIFIED_NOT_INVENTED"],
ln["Forbidden / noncanonical values","EXCLUDED"],
ln["Values not generated by this step","IDENTIFIED"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"AUTHORIZED INPUTS",
"- STEP 0W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 1W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 2W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 3W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 4W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 5W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 6W PASS_LOCKED",

"- RFC SU v1 Reproducibility Metadata and Wolfram Ledger, Section 1",
"- RFC-SU RFC SU v1 Architecture Ledger latest",
"",
"FORBIDDEN INPUTS / METHODS"
},
Map["- "<>#&,forbidden],
{
"",
"FIRST ACTION",
ln["globalFirstAction",firstAction],
ln["weightedFirstAction",weightedFirstAction],
"",
"TRIAD-WEIGHT DECOMPOSITION",
ln["qvCore",num[decomp["qvCore"]]],
ln["darkKernelBridge",num[decomp["darkKernelBridge"]]],
ln["cifPower",num[decomp["cifPower"]]],
ln["rflPower",num[decomp["rflPower"]]],
ln["CIFWeight",num[weights["CIF"]]],
ln["QVWeight",num[weights["QV"]]],
ln["RFLWeight",num[weights["RFL"]]],
ln["weightSum",num[Total[Values[weights]]]],
"",
"STEP 9W CARRIED GROWTH / GEOMETRY INPUTS",
ln["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore",num[step9["primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["growthDisciplineScore",num[step9["growthDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[step9["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedGrowthBoundaryMarker"]]],
ln["weightedMatterCouplingMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedMatterCouplingMarker"]]],
ln["weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier",num[growthCarry["weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier"]]],
ln["weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedGeometryCoherenceMarker"]]],
ln["weightedFractalCoherenceMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedFractalCoherenceMarker"]]],
ln["weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker",num[growthCarry["weightedInternalSigmaSeedMarker"]]],
ln["sigma8ProxyInternal",num[growthCarry["sigma8ProxyInternal"]]],
ln["S8ProxyInternal",num[growthCarry["S8ProxyInternal"]]],
"",
"STEP 8W / STEP 7W STRUCTURE-CARRY INPUTS",
ln["expansionPersistenceScore",num[step8["expansionPersistenceScore"]]],
ln["transferBridgeReadinessScore",num[step7["transferBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker",num[transferCarry["weightedMatterCouplingSeedMarker"]]],
ln["weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker",num[expansionCarry["weightedDarkMatterCompressionDecayMarker"]]],
ln["weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker",num[expansionCarry["weightedDistanceSeedReadinessMarker"]]],
"",
"INTERNAL WEIGHTED HALO / GALAXY / NONLINEAR MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,haloMarkers],
{
"",
"HALO / GALAXY / NONLINEAR BRIDGE SCORES",
ln["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore",num[scores["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["nonlinearDisciplineScore",num[scores["nonlinearDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[scores["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["sourceCarryCompleteness",num[scores["sourceCarryCompleteness"]]],

ln["notApplicableDiscipline",num[scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]]],
"",
"HALO / GALAXY STATE BOUNDARIES",
ln["moduleStatus",haloState["moduleStatus"]],
ln["haloMassFunction",haloState["haloMassFunction"]],
ln["nonlinearMatterPower",haloState["nonlinearMatterPower"]],
ln["mergerTree",haloState["mergerTree"]],

},

},

{

"NO-RETUNE / NO-FIT FIREWALL",
ln["Deleted prior notebook used",yn[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]]],
ln["Public data used during generation",yn[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]]],
ln["Parameter search performed",yn[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]]],
ln["Curve fitting performed",yn[viol["curveFitPerformed"]]],
ln["Post-hoc correction performed",yn[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]]],
