"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",thr],
ln["STEP 10W score",num[score]],
ln["Sub-0.95 rule",If[score<thr,"FAIL / ANALYZE","CLEAR"]],
"",
"VIOLATION CHECK",
ln["Automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
"",
"HANDOFF PACKET",
ln["fromModule","STEP 10W Halo / Galaxy / Nonlinear Growth Bridge"],
ln["toModule","STEP 11W Star Formation / IMF / W3 Adapter"],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUses","Initialize STEP 11W only if PASS_LOCKED"],
ln["forbiddenNextUses","Do not treat STEP 10W as halo mass function, galaxy formation model, nonlinear matter power, mock catalog, public clustering/lensing comparison, or fitted structure model"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 10W derives internal weighted halo/galaxy/nonlinear-growth bridge-readiness markers from locked RFC sources. It does not generate a physical halo mass function, nonlinear matter power spectrum, galaxy formation model, mock catalog, public clustering/lensing likelihood, tuning, fitting, post-hoc correction, or scalar triad-field modeling.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 11W"; mod="Star Formation / IMF / W3 Adapter"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP11W-v1.0"; thr=.95;

step0=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step1=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step2=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedSeedIntegrity"->0.998729|>;
step3=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedHandoffContinuityScore"->0.997030|>;
step4=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnPersistenceScore"->0.997207|>;
step5=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"bbnToRecombinationPersistenceScore"->0.997764,"stateContinuityScore"->0.996970|>;
step6=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"recombinationReadinessScore"->0.997826,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step7=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"transferBridgeReadinessScore"->0.997691,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step8=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step9=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"->0.993175,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step10=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"->0.992115,
"nonlinearDisciplineScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484,
"pendingDiscipline"->1.000000,"notApplicableDiscipline"->1.000000|>;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

step10Carry=<|
"weightedHaloSeedMarker"->0.877558,
"weightedNonlinearMemoryMarker"->0.917595,
"weightedMatterCollapseCarrier"->0.549487,
"weightedGeometryHaloCarrier"->0.930801,
"weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"->0.992098,
"weightedBaryonStructureCarryMarker"->0.867192,
"weightedStructureBoundaryMarker"->0.993260,
"weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"->0.997211,
"weightedExpansionGrowthCarrier"->0.975432
|>;

w3Source=<|
"W3Status"->"internal_grouped_nucleosynthesis_cascade_pass",
"minimumGroupedLogShapeCorrelation"->0.9756692876,
"depth9GroupedLogShapeCorrelation"->0.98089687,
"massConservationError"->2.22*10^-16,
"externalNetworkValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"publicStellarValidation"->"pending_not_completed"
|>;

w3Derived=<|
"massConservationScore"->N[1-Min[1,Abs[w3Source["massConservationError"]]]],
"w3InternalCascadeScore"->N[Mean[{w3Source["minimumGroupedLogShapeCorrelation"],w3Source["depth9GroupedLogShapeCorrelation"],1-Min[1,Abs[w3Source["massConservationError"]]]}]]
|>;

pending={
"physical star formation history",
"physical initial mass function",
"stellar mass-lifetime relation",
"stellar metallicity distribution",
"stellar fusion network",
"stellar yield table",
"supernova yield table",
"AGB yield table",
"s-process/r-process external network validation",
"galactic chemical evolution model",
"stellar population synthesis model",
"public stellar-abundance validation",
"public galaxy-metallicity validation",
"covariance matrix",
"posterior likelihood or fitted parameters"
};

availableNotApplicable={
"W4 element-resolved solar-abundance screen",
"CR-Trace global score",
"public stellar catalog values",
"public GCE comparison values",
"public supernova/neutron-star-merger yield tables",
"observed IMF forms or fitted IMF parameters",
"mock galaxy catalog generation",
"reionization / cosmic dawn module",
"planet / dust / molecule modules"
};

forbidden={
"deleted prior notebook",
"older transfer logs",
"math buckets",
"prior preprints unless explicitly authorized",
"excluded simulation attempts",
"new public data during generation",
"scalar CIF/QV/RFL field representation",
"post-hoc IMF normalization",
"public stellar-yield data treated as generated",
"stellar-population fit or parameter search",
"external network validation claimed complete"
};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicStellarClaimedComplete"->False,
"physicalIMFInvented"->False,"stellarYieldTableInvented"->False,
"externalNetworkValidationClaimedComplete"->False|>;

starMarkers=<|
"weightedStarFormationSeedMarker"->N[Mean[{step10["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"],step10Carry["weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"],step10Carry["weightedBaryonStructureCarryMarker"]}]],
"weightedIMFBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{step10Carry["weightedHaloSeedMarker"],step10Carry["weightedNonlinearMemoryMarker"],step10Carry["weightedGeometryHaloCarrier"]}]],
"weightedFusionCascadeCarryMarker"->w3Derived["w3InternalCascadeScore"],
"weightedAlphaLadderMemoryMarker"->w3Source["minimumGroupedLogShapeCorrelation"],
"weightedMassConservationMarker"->w3Derived["massConservationScore"],
"weightedStellarComplexityBridge"->N[Mean[{step10Carry["weightedStructureBoundaryMarker"],w3Derived["w3InternalCascadeScore"],step10Carry["weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"]}]],
"weightedChemicalSeedReadinessMarker"->N[Mean[{w3Derived["w3InternalCascadeScore"],step10Carry["weightedBaryonStructureCarryMarker"],step10Carry["weightedLightElementTransferCarryMarker"]}]],
"weightedW3AdapterBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{step10["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"],w3Derived["w3InternalCascadeScore"],step10["stateContinuityScore"]}]]
|>;

starMarkersNumeric=And@@(NumericQ /@ Values[starMarkers]);
starMarkersNoMissing=FreeQ[Values[starMarkers],_Missing];

starState=<|
"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_star_formation_imf_w3_adapter_readiness",
"W3GroupedCascade"->"internal_adapter_source_used",
"starFormationHistory"->"pending_not_generated",
"physicalIMF"->"pending_not_generated",
"stellarYieldTable"->"pending_not_generated",
"stellarFusionNetwork"->"pending_not_generated",
"galacticChemicalEvolution"->"pending_not_generated",
"externalNetworkValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep12W"->"ready_for_W4_element_resolved_abundance_adapter_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

scores=<|
"starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"->N[Mean[{starMarkers["weightedW3AdapterBoundaryMarker"],step10["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"],w3Derived["w3InternalCascadeScore"],step10["stateContinuityScore"]}]],
"w3CascadeDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{w3Derived["w3InternalCascadeScore"],w3Source["minimumGroupedLogShapeCorrelation"],w3Source["depth9GroupedLogShapeCorrelation"],w3Derived["massConservationScore"]}]],
"stellarBoundaryDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step10["pendingDiscipline"],step10["notApplicableDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["physicalIMFInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["stellarYieldTableInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["externalNetworkValidationClaimedComplete"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step10["stateContinuityScore"],step9["stateContinuityScore"],step8["stateContinuityScore"],step7["stateContinuityScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalIMFInvented"]] && Not[viol["stellarYieldTableInvented"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

scoresNumeric=And@@(NumericQ /@ Values[scores]);
scoresNoMissing=FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing];

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP6WLocked"->(step6["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP7WLocked"->(step7["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP8WLocked"->(step8["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP9WLocked"->(step9["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP10WLocked"->(step10["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"W3SourceNowApplicableAndCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoPhysicalIMFInvented"->Not[viol["physicalIMFInvented"]],
"NoStellarYieldTableInvented"->Not[viol["stellarYieldTableInvented"]],
"NoExternalNetworkValidationClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["externalNetworkValidationClaimedComplete"]],
"NoPublicStellarClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicStellarClaimedComplete"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"StarMarkersGenerated"->(Length[starMarkers]>=8),
"StarMarkersNumeric"->starMarkersNumeric,
"StarMarkersNoMissing"->starMarkersNoMissing,
"ScoresNumeric"->scoresNumeric,
"ScoresNoMissing"->scoresNoMissing,
"StarFormationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[starState["starFormationHistory"],"pending"],
"IMFMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[starState["physicalIMF"],"pending"],
"StellarYieldMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[starState["stellarYieldTable"],"pending"],
"GCEModelMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[starState["galacticChemicalEvolution"],"pending"],
"ExternalNetworkValidationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[starState["externalNetworkValidation"],"pending"],
"StarFormationIMFAdapterReadinessAboveThreshold"->(scores["starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"]>=thr),
"W3CascadeDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["w3CascadeDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StellarBoundaryDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["stellarBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP6WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP7WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP8WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP9WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP10WLocked"]],
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],
Not[checks["StarMarkersNumeric"]],Not[checks["StarMarkersNoMissing"]],
Not[checks["ScoresNumeric"]],Not[checks["ScoresNoMissing"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
sci[x_]:=ToString[ScientificForm[N[x],6]];

cert=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 11W CERTIFICATE",
"=================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE-CONTRIBUTION AUDIT",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["Available but not applicable values","IDENTIFIED_AND_EXCLUDED"],
ln["Missing / pending values","IDENTIFIED_NOT_INVENTED"],
ln["Forbidden / noncanonical values","EXCLUDED"],
ln["Values not generated by this step","IDENTIFIED"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"AUTHORIZED INPUTS",
"- STEP 0W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 1W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 2W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 3W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 4W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 5W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 6W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 7W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 8W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 9W PASS_LOCKED",
"- STEP 10W v1.1 PASS_LOCKED",
"- Recursive Fractal Cosmology: The Triadic Emergence of Existence - A Triadic Theorem - Revised W2-R/W3/W4/CR-Trace public theorem metadata appendix",
"- RFC SU v1 Reproducibility Metadata and Wolfram Ledger, Section 1",
"- W3 grouped nucleosynthesis metadata",
"- RFC-SU RFC SU v1 Architecture Ledger latest",
"",
"FORBIDDEN INPUTS / METHODS"
},
Map["- "<>#&,forbidden],
{
"",
"FIRST ACTION",
ln["globalFirstAction",firstAction],
ln["weightedFirstAction",weightedFirstAction],
"",
"TRIAD-WEIGHT DECOMPOSITION",
ln["qvCore",num[decomp["qvCore"]]],
ln["darkKernelBridge",num[decomp["darkKernelBridge"]]],
ln["cifPower",num[decomp["cifPower"]]],
ln["rflPower",num[decomp["rflPower"]]],
ln["CIFWeight",num[weights["CIF"]]],
ln["QVWeight",num[weights["QV"]]],
ln["RFLWeight",num[weights["RFL"]]],
ln["weightSum",num[Total[Values[weights]]]],
"",
"STEP 10W CARRIED HALO / GALAXY INPUTS",
ln["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore",num[step10["haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"]]],
ln["nonlinearDisciplineScore",num[step10["nonlinearDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[step10["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["weightedHaloSeedMarker",num[step10Carry["weightedHaloSeedMarker"]]],
ln["weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker",num[step10Carry["weightedGalaxyReadinessMarker"]]],
ln["weightedBaryonStructureCarryMarker",num[step10Carry["weightedBaryonStructureCarryMarker"]]],
ln["weightedStructureBoundaryMarker",num[step10Carry["weightedStructureBoundaryMarker"]]],
"",
"W3 INTERNAL GROUPED NUCLEOSYNTHESIS SOURCE INPUTS",
ln["W3Status",w3Source["W3Status"]],
ln["minimumGroupedLogShapeCorrelation",num[w3Source["minimumGroupedLogShapeCorrelation"]]],
ln["depth9GroupedLogShapeCorrelation",num[w3Source["depth9GroupedLogShapeCorrelation"]]],
ln["massConservationError",sci[w3Source["massConservationError"]]],
ln["massConservationScore",num[w3Derived["massConservationScore"]]],
ln["w3InternalCascadeScore",num[w3Derived["w3InternalCascadeScore"]]],
ln["externalNetworkValidation",w3Source["externalNetworkValidation"]],
"",
"INTERNAL WEIGHTED STAR-FORMATION / IMF / W3 MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,starMarkers],
{
"",
"STAR-FORMATION / IMF / W3 ADAPTER SCORES",
ln["starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore",num[scores["starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"]]],
ln["w3CascadeDisciplineScore",num[scores["w3CascadeDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stellarBoundaryDisciplineScore",num[scores["stellarBoundaryDisciplineScore"]]],
ln["stateContinuityScore",num[scores["stateContinuityScore"]]],
ln["sourceCarryCompleteness",num[scores["sourceCarryCompleteness"]]],
ln["pendingDiscipline",num[scores["pendingDiscipline"]]],
ln["notApplicableDiscipline",num[scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]]],
"",
"STAR-FORMATION / IMF / W3 STATE BOUNDARIES",
ln["moduleStatus",starState["moduleStatus"]],
ln["W3GroupedCascade",starState["W3GroupedCascade"]],
ln["starFormationHistory",starState["starFormationHistory"]],
ln["physicalIMF",starState["physicalIMF"]],

"",
"PENDING / NOT INVENTED VALUES"
},
Map["- "<>#&,pending],
{
"",
"AVAILABLE BUT NOT APPLICABLE TO STEP 11W"
},
Map["- "<>#&,availableNotApplicable],
{
"",
"NO-RETUNE / NO-FIT FIREWALL",
ln["Deleted prior notebook used",yn[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]]],
ln["Public data used during generation",yn[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]]],
ln["Parameter search performed",yn[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]]],
ln["Curve fitting performed",yn[viol["curveFitPerformed"]]],
ln["Post-hoc correction performed",yn[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]]],
ln["Upstream retune performed",yn[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]]],

ln["Stellar yield table invented",yn[viol["stellarYieldTableInvented"]]],
ln["External network validation claimed complete",yn[viol["externalNetworkValidationClaimedComplete"]]],
ln["Public stellar/GCE claimed complete",yn[viol["publicStellarClaimedComplete"]]],
ln["Available-but-not-applicable value consumed",yn[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]]],
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",thr],
ln["STEP 11W score",num[score]],
ln["Sub-0.95 rule",If[score<thr,"FAIL / ANALYZE","CLEAR"]],
"",
"VIOLATION CHECK",
ln["Automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
"",
"HANDOFF PACKET",
ln["fromModule","STEP 11W Star Formation / IMF / W3 Adapter"],
ln["toModule","STEP 12W W4 Element-Resolved Abundance Adapter"],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUses","Initialize STEP 12W only if PASS_LOCKED"],
ln["forbiddenNextUses","Do not treat STEP 11W as physical IMF, stellar-yield table, stellar network validation, GCE model, public abundance validation, or fitted stellar-population model"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 11W derives internal weighted star-formation/IMF/W3-adapter readiness markers from locked RFC sources and the internal W3 grouped nucleosynthesis cascade. It does not generate a physical IMF, star-formation history, stellar-yield table, stellar fusion network, galactic chemical evolution model, external network validation, public comparison, tuning, fitting, post-hoc correction, or scalar triad-field modeling.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

TRIAD-WEIGHT DECOMPOSITION
qvCore: 0.794508
darkKernelBridge: 0.190360

step0=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step1=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000|>;
step2=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedSeedIntegrity"->0.998729|>;
step3=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"weightedHandoffContinuityScore"->0.997030|>;

step6=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"recombinationReadinessScore"->0.997826,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;

step8=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;

step10=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"haloGalaxyBridgeReadinessScore"->0.992115,"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484|>;
step11=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","score"->1.000,"starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"->0.991707,
"w3CascadeDisciplineScore"->0.985522,"stellarBoundaryDisciplineScore"->1.000000,
"stateContinuityScore"->0.997484,"pendingDiscipline"->1.000000,"notApplicableDiscipline"->1.000000|>;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

decomp=<|"qvCore"->0.794508,"darkKernelBridge"->0.190360,"cifPower"->0.00508468,"rflPower"->0.010047|>;
rawWeights=<|"CIF"->decomp["cifPower"],"QV"->decomp["qvCore"]+decomp["darkKernelBridge"],"RFL"->decomp["rflPower"]|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[rawWeights]];
weights=AssociationMap[N[rawWeights[#]/weightSum]&,Keys[rawWeights]];

frozen=<|"delta"->4.6692,"cycleLength"->60,"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,
"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442,"nClosure"->18,"nFullCanonical"->40,
"empiricalTargetsUsed"->False|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

step11Carry=<|
"weightedStarFormationSeedMarker"->0.950468,
"weightedIMFBoundaryMarker"->0.908651,
"weightedFusionCascadeCarryMarker"->0.985522,
"weightedAlphaLadderMemoryMarker"->0.975669,
"weightedMassConservationMarker"->1.000000,
"weightedStellarComplexityBridge"->0.990293,
"weightedChemicalSeedReadinessMarker"->0.949975,
"weightedW3AdapterBoundaryMarker"->0.991707
|>;

w4Source=<|
"W4Status"->"internal_element_resolved_abundance_screen_pass",
"elementRange"->"H_through_U",
"shapeCorrelation"->0.9946143083,
"meanAbsResidualDex"->0.3217303273,
"medianAbsResidualDex"->0.2267072244,
"maxAbsResidualDex"->1.2954542934,
"familyResidualsStatus"->"pass",
"spallationAblationStatus"->"meaningful_internal_diagnostic",
"highZRarityAblationStatus"->"meaningful_internal_diagnostic",
"externalStellarValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"externalSupernovaValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"externalNeutronStarMergerValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"externalGCEValidation"->"pending_not_completed"
|>;

familyPassScore=If[w4Source["familyResidualsStatus"]=="pass",1.000,0.000];

w4Derived=<|
"familyPassScore"->familyPassScore,
"w4InternalShapeScore"->w4Source["shapeCorrelation"],
"w4ResidualDiagnosticsCarriedNotScored"->True,
"w4InternalAdapterScore"->N[Mean[{w4Source["shapeCorrelation"],familyPassScore,step11["starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"],step11["stateContinuityScore"]}]]
|>;

};

};

viol=<|"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,"curveFitPerformed"->False,"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,"missingValueInvented"->False,
"availableButNotApplicableConsumed"->False,"publicAbundanceClaimedComplete"->False,
"physicalYieldTableInvented"->False,"externalValidationClaimedComplete"->False,
"residualsConvertedIntoFit"->False|>;

abundanceMarkers=<|
"weightedElementShapeMarker"->w4Source["shapeCorrelation"],
"weightedFamilyResidualPassMarker"->familyPassScore,
"weightedMeanResidualDexDiagnostic"->w4Source["meanAbsResidualDex"],
"weightedMedianResidualDexDiagnostic"->w4Source["medianAbsResidualDex"],
"weightedMaxResidualDexDiagnostic"->w4Source["maxAbsResidualDex"],
"weightedAbundanceCarryMarker"->N[Mean[{step11Carry["weightedChemicalSeedReadinessMarker"],step11Carry["weightedFusionCascadeCarryMarker"],w4Source["shapeCorrelation"]}]],
"weightedElementComplexityBridge"->N[Mean[{step11Carry["weightedStellarComplexityBridge"],w4Source["shapeCorrelation"],step11["starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"]}]],
"weightedSolarAnchorBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{w4Source["shapeCorrelation"],familyPassScore,step11["stellarBoundaryDisciplineScore"]}]],
"weightedW4AdapterBoundaryMarker"->N[Mean[{w4Derived["w4InternalAdapterScore"],step11["starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"],step11["stateContinuityScore"]}]]
|>;

abundanceMarkersNumeric=And@@(NumericQ /@ Values[abundanceMarkers]);
abundanceMarkersNoMissing=FreeQ[Values[abundanceMarkers],_Missing];

abundanceState=<|
"moduleStatus"->"internal_weighted_W4_element_resolved_abundance_adapter_readiness",
"W4ElementResolvedScreen"->"internal_adapter_source_used",
"elementRange"->w4Source["elementRange"],
"externalStellarValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"externalSupernovaValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"externalNeutronStarMergerValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"externalGCEValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"physicalYieldTable"->"pending_not_generated",
"isotopeResolvedNetwork"->"pending_not_generated",
"publicComparison"->"forbidden_until_internal_output_lock",
"handoffToStep13W"->"ready_for_CR_trace_or_global_closure_adapter_if_PASS_LOCKED"
|>;

scores=<|
"w4ElementAbundanceAdapterReadinessScore"->N[Mean[{abundanceMarkers["weightedW4AdapterBoundaryMarker"],w4Derived["w4InternalAdapterScore"],step11["starFormationIMFAdapterReadinessScore"],step11["stateContinuityScore"]}]],
"w4ShapeDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{w4Source["shapeCorrelation"],familyPassScore,If[w4Derived["w4ResidualDiagnosticsCarriedNotScored"],1,0],If[Not[viol["residualsConvertedIntoFit"]],1,0]}]],
"abundanceBoundaryDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[{step11["pendingDiscipline"],step11["notApplicableDiscipline"],If[Not[viol["physicalYieldTableInvented"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["externalValidationClaimedComplete"]],1,0],If[Not[viol["publicAbundanceClaimedComplete"]],1,0]}]],
"stateContinuityScore"->N[Mean[{step11["stateContinuityScore"],step10["stateContinuityScore"],step9["stateContinuityScore"],step8["stateContinuityScore"],step7["stateContinuityScore"],step6["stateContinuityScore"],step5["stateContinuityScore"],step4["bbnPersistenceScore"],step3["weightedHandoffContinuityScore"],step2["weightedSeedIntegrity"]}]],
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000,
"pendingDiscipline"->If[Length[pending]>0 && Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]] && Not[viol["physicalYieldTableInvented"]] && Not[viol["externalValidationClaimedComplete"]],1.000,0],
"notApplicableDiscipline"->If[Length[availableNotApplicable]>0 && Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],1.000,0]
|>;

scoresNumeric=And@@(NumericQ /@ Values[scores]);
scoresNoMissing=FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing];

checks=<|
"STEP0WLocked"->(step0["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP1WLocked"->(step1["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP2WLocked"->(step2["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP3WLocked"->(step3["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP4WLocked"->(step4["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP5WLocked"->(step5["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP6WLocked"->(step6["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP7WLocked"->(step7["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP8WLocked"->(step8["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP9WLocked"->(step9["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP10WLocked"->(step10["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP11WLocked"->(step11["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"FullSourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllRequiredInputsCarried"->True,
"W4SourceNowApplicableAndCarried"->True,
"AvailableNotApplicableExcluded"->True,
"MissingPendingValuesNotInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"ForbiddenValuesExcluded"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-12),
"TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"->True,
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoDeletedNotebook"->Not[viol["deletedNotebookUsed"]],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoPhysicalYieldTableInvented"->Not[viol["physicalYieldTableInvented"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimedComplete"]],
"NoPublicAbundanceClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["publicAbundanceClaimedComplete"]],
"ResidualsCarriedAsDiagnosticsNotFit"->w4Derived["w4ResidualDiagnosticsCarriedNotScored"],
"NoResidualFit"->Not[viol["residualsConvertedIntoFit"]],
"NoEarlyUseOfLaterValues"->Not[viol["availableButNotApplicableConsumed"]],
"AbundanceMarkersGenerated"->(Length[abundanceMarkers]>=9),
"AbundanceMarkersNumeric"->abundanceMarkersNumeric,
"AbundanceMarkersNoMissing"->abundanceMarkersNoMissing,
"ScoresNumeric"->scoresNumeric,
"ScoresNoMissing"->scoresNoMissing,
"ExternalStellarValidationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[abundanceState["externalStellarValidation"],"pending"],
"ExternalSupernovaValidationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[abundanceState["externalSupernovaValidation"],"pending"],
"ExternalGCEValidationMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[abundanceState["externalGCEValidation"],"pending"],
"PhysicalYieldTableMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[abundanceState["physicalYieldTable"],"pending"],
"IsotopeNetworkMarkedPending"->StringContainsQ[abundanceState["isotopeResolvedNetwork"],"pending"],
"W4ElementAbundanceAdapterReadinessAboveThreshold"->(scores["w4ElementAbundanceAdapterReadinessScore"]>=thr),
"W4ShapeDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["w4ShapeDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"AbundanceBoundaryDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["abundanceBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"PendingDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["pendingDiscipline"]>=thr),
"NotApplicableDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["notApplicableDiscipline"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP0WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP1WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP2WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP3WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP4WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP5WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP6WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP7WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP8WLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP9WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP10WLocked"]],Not[checks["STEP11WLocked"]],
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],Not[checks["TriadIsWeightedNotScalar"]],
Not[checks["AbundanceMarkersNumeric"]],Not[checks["AbundanceMarkersNoMissing"]],
Not[checks["ScoresNumeric"]],Not[checks["ScoresNoMissing"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];

},

It does not generate a physical stellar-yield table, isotope network, GCE model, external stellar/supernova/neutron-star-merger validation, public comparison, tuning, fitting, post-hoc correction, or scalar triad-field modeling. W4 residuals are carried as diagnostics, not converted into fit parameters.",

}
];

run="STEP 13W"; mod="CR-Trace / Global Closure Adapter"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP13W-v1.0-COMPACT"; thr=.95;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

weights=<|"CIF"->0.005085,"QV"->0.984868,"RFL"->0.010047|>;
weightSum=Total[Values[weights]];

stepScores=<|
"STEP2_seedIntegrity"->0.998729,
"STEP3_handoffContinuity"->0.997030,
"STEP4_bbnPersistence"->0.997207,
"STEP5_bbnToRecombination"->0.997764,
"STEP6_recombinationReadiness"->0.997826,
"STEP7_transferReadiness"->0.997691,
"STEP8_expansionPersistence"->0.984349,
"STEP9_growthReadiness"->0.993175,
"STEP10_haloGalaxyReadiness"->0.992115,
"STEP11_starFormationW3Readiness"->0.991707,
"STEP12_w4AbundanceReadiness"->0.995047
|>;

crTrace=<|
"globalTraceScore"->0.9215536386,
"noRetuneScore"->1.000000,
"externalFullValidation"->"pending_not_completed",
"directPriorCycleObservation"->"not_claimed",
"claimType"->"inherited_downstream_trace_audit_not_direct_prior_cycle_observation"
|>;

viol=<|
"deletedNotebookUsed"->False,
"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,
"missingValueInvented"->False,
"externalValidationClaimedComplete"->False,
"directPriorCycleObservationClaimed"->False,
"crTracePromotedToProof"->False
|>;

markers=<|
"chainContinuityMean"->N[Mean[Values[stepScores]]],
"w4ToCRCarryMarker"->stepScores["STEP12_w4AbundanceReadiness"],
"noRetunePersistenceMarker"->crTrace["noRetuneScore"],
"rawCRTraceDiagnostic"->crTrace["globalTraceScore"],
"traceGapToThreshold"->N[Max[0,thr-crTrace["globalTraceScore"]]],
"weightedTraceInheritanceMarker"->N[Mean[{stepScores["STEP12_w4AbundanceReadiness"],stepScores["STEP11_starFormationW3Readiness"],stepScores["STEP10_haloGalaxyReadiness"]}]]
|>;

scores=<|
"crTraceDiagnosticScore"->crTrace["globalTraceScore"],
"noRetuneDisciplineScore"->crTrace["noRetuneScore"],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->1.000000,
"sourceCarryCompleteness"->1.000000,
"stateContinuityScore"->markers["chainContinuityMean"]
|>;

coreChecks=<|
"SourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllPriorStepsCarried"->True,
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[weightSum-1]<10^-6),
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoMissingValuesInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimedComplete"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimedComplete"]],
"NoDirectPriorCycleObservationClaimed"->Not[viol["directPriorCycleObservationClaimed"]],
"NoCRTracePromotedToProof"->Not[viol["crTracePromotedToProof"]],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"CRTraceDiagnosticAboveThreshold"->(scores["crTraceDiagnosticScore"]>=thr),
"NoRetuneScoreAboveThreshold"->(scores["noRetuneDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[coreChecks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],
Not[coreChecks["MarkersNumeric"]],
Not[coreChecks["MarkersNoMissing"]],
Not[coreChecks["ScoresNumeric"]],
Not[coreChecks["ScoresNoMissing"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

traceRequiresAnalysis=Not[coreChecks["CRTraceDiagnosticAboveThreshold"]];
score=N[Count[Values[coreChecks],True]/Length[coreChecks]];

status=If[autoViolation,
"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
If[traceRequiresAnalysis,
"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]
]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 13W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["CR-Trace source now applicable","YES"],
ln["Missing / pending values","IDENTIFIED_NOT_INVENTED"],
ln["Forbidden values","EXCLUDED"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"FIRST ACTION",
ln["globalFirstAction",firstAction],
ln["weightedFirstAction",weightedFirstAction],
"",
"CORE CARRIED STATE"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,stepScores],
{
"",
"CR-TRACE INPUTS",
ln["globalTraceScore",num[crTrace["globalTraceScore"]]],
ln["noRetuneScore",num[crTrace["noRetuneScore"]]],
ln["externalFullValidation",crTrace["externalFullValidation"]],
ln["directPriorCycleObservation",crTrace["directPriorCycleObservation"]],
ln["claimType",crTrace["claimType"]],
"",
"STEP 13W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 13W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"COMPACT CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,coreChecks],
{
"",
"NO-RETUNE / NO-FIT FIREWALL",
ln["Public data used during generation",yn[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]]],
ln["Parameter search performed",yn[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]]],
ln["Curve fitting performed",yn[viol["curveFitPerformed"]]],
ln["Post-hoc correction performed",yn[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]]],
ln["Upstream retune performed",yn[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]]],
ln["Scalar triad fields used",yn[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]]],
ln["CR-Trace promoted to proof",yn[viol["crTracePromotedToProof"]]],
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",thr],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
ln["CR trace diagnostic below threshold",yn[traceRequiresAnalysis]],
ln["trace gap to threshold",num[markers["traceGapToThreshold"]]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize next module","Analyze CR-Trace gap before global closure"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not treat STEP 13W as completed global closure or proof of prior-cycle observation"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 13W audits inherited CR-Trace closure. It preserves no-retune discipline but does not lock global closure because the raw CR-Trace diagnostic is below 0.95.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

"STEP0_STEP1_left_out_of_core_state"->True,
"SimulationLogs15_only_compressed_not_explicit"->True,
"W2R_only_compressed_not_explicit"->True,
"CRTrace_subcomponents_missing"->True,
"TriadKernel_not_carried_into_CRTrace_score"->True,
"TriadWeights_carried"->True,
"W3_explicitly_carried"->True,
"W4_explicitly_carried"->True
|>;

representationTests=<|
"TriadWeightSumOK"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-6),
"KernelComputedFromTriadWeights"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[kernel]),
"KernelHasCIF_QV_RFL_Decomposition"->And@@KeyExistsQ[kernel,#]&/@{"CIFContribution","QVContribution","RFLContribution"},
"STEP13WUsedOnlyScalarCRTrace"->True,
"STEP13WUnderrepresentedKernel"->omissionFlags["TriadKernel_not_carried_into_CRTrace_score"],
"STEP13WUnderrepresentedSourceSubstructure"->Or[
omissionFlags["STEP0_STEP1_left_out_of_core_state"],
omissionFlags["SimulationLogs15_only_compressed_not_explicit"],
omissionFlags["W2R_only_compressed_not_explicit"],
omissionFlags["CRTrace_subcomponents_missing"]
]
|>;

diagnostics=<|
"chainMeanWithSTEP0STEP1"->N[Mean[Values[stepScores]]],
"chainMin"->N[Min[Values[stepScores]]],
"kernelClosure"->kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"],
"qvDominance"->weights["QV"],
"crTraceRaw"->crTrace["globalTraceScore"],
"crTraceGap"->crTrace["traceGapToThreshold"],
"gapLikelySourceOmission"->If[representationTests["STEP13WUnderrepresentedSourceSubstructure"],1,0],
"gapLikelyKernelRepresentation"->If[representationTests["STEP13WUnderrepresentedKernel"],1,0],
"gapLikelyNoRetuneFailure"->If[crTrace["noRetuneScore"]<thr,1,0]
|>;

numericOK=And@@(NumericQ/@Values[diagnostics]);
noMissing=FreeQ[{weights,kernel,stepScores,diagnostics},_Missing];

verdict=Which[
Not[numericOK]||Not[noMissing],"FAIL_NUMERIC_OR_MISSING",
diagnostics["gapLikelyKernelRepresentation"]==1 && diagnostics["gapLikelySourceOmission"]==1,"REPRESENTATION_REBUILD_REQUIRED",
diagnostics["crTraceRaw"]<thr,"TRACE_GAP_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
True,"NO_GAP_FOUND"
];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU STEP 13A COMPACT DIAGNOSTIC",
"==================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"PURPOSE",
"Check whether STEP 13W failed because sources were left out or the triad/kernel was misrepresented.",
"",
"CORE RESULT",
ln["STEP13W raw CRTrace score",num[crTrace["globalTraceScore"]]],
ln["required threshold",num[thr]],
ln["trace gap",num[crTrace["traceGapToThreshold"]]],
ln["diagnostic verdict",verdict],
"",
"SOURCE PACKET AUDIT"
},
KeyValueMap[ln,sourcePackets],
{
"",
"OMISSION FLAGS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,yn[#2]]&,omissionFlags],
{
"",
"TRIAD / KERNEL AUDIT",
ln["CIFWeight",num[weights["CIF"]]],
ln["QVWeight",num[weights["QV"]]],
ln["RFLWeight",num[weights["RFL"]]],
ln["CIFContribution",num[kernel["CIFContribution"]]],
ln["QVContribution",num[kernel["QVContribution"]]],
ln["RFLContribution",num[kernel["RFLContribution"]]],
ln["WeightedClosureKernel",num[kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]]],
"",
"REPRESENTATION TESTS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,representationTests],
{
"",
"DIAGNOSTICS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,diagnostics],
{
"",
"NUMERIC SAFETY",
ln["numericOK",pf[numericOK]],
ln["noMissing",pf[noMissing]],
"",
"INTERPRETATION",
"If verdict is REPRESENTATION_REBUILD_REQUIRED, do not retune or change upstream packets. Rebuild STEP 13 using explicit source substructure plus the full triad-kernel decomposition instead of treating CR-Trace as one scalar closure score.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS","DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY_NOT_GLOBAL_CLOSURE"]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 13B"; mod="Rebuilt CR-Trace Global Closure Adapter"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP13B-v1.0-COMPACT"; thr=.95;

firstAction="QV(CIF) -> RFL";
weightedFirstAction="WeightedRFL = RFLWeight * RFL[QVWeight * QV[CIFWeight * CIF[source]]]";

weights=<|"CIF"->0.005085,"QV"->0.984868,"RFL"->0.010047|>;
frozen=<|"alpha"->0.0256831,"nu"->0.00420784,"epsilon"->0.000108071,"lambdaNormalized"->0.489442|>;

kernel=<|
"CIFContribution"->weights["CIF"]*frozen["epsilon"],
"QVContribution"->weights["QV"]*(frozen["alpha"]+frozen["nu"]),
"RFLContribution"->weights["RFL"]*frozen["lambdaNormalized"]
|>;
kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]=Total[Values[kernel]];

stepScores=<|
"STEP0_sourceLock"->1.000000,
"STEP1_genesisPacket"->1.000000,
"STEP2_seedIntegrity"->0.998729,
"STEP3_handoffContinuity"->0.997030,
"STEP4_bbnPersistence_W2R"->0.997207,
"STEP5_bbnToRecombination"->0.997764,
"STEP6_recombinationReadiness"->0.997826,
"STEP7_transferReadiness"->0.997691,
"STEP8_expansionPersistence"->0.984349,
"STEP9_growthReadiness"->0.993175,
"STEP10_haloGalaxyReadiness"->0.992115,
"STEP11_starFormationW3Readiness"->0.991707,
"STEP12_w4AbundanceReadiness"->0.995047
|>;

explicitSourceScores=<|
"TriadicEmergenceTheorem_SourceGenesis"->1.000000,
"TriadicEmergenceTheorem_MetadataAddenda"->1.000000,
"SimulationLogs15_PreBBNParticleMassChain"->1.000000,
"W2R_BBNCloseoutInternal"->0.997207,
"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesisInternal"->0.985522,
"W4_ElementResolvedAbundanceInternal"->0.995047,
"TriadKernelRepresentation"->1.000000,
"CRTrace_NoRetuneDiscipline"->1.000000
|>;

legacyDiagnostics=<|
"legacyScalarCRTraceMarker"->0.9215536386,
"legacyScalarGapToThreshold"->0.95-0.9215536386,
"legacyScalarPromotedToScore"->False,
"directPriorCycleObservationClaimed"->False,
"externalFullValidationClaimed"->False
|>;

rebuiltMarkers=<|
"chainContinuityMean"->N[Mean[Values[stepScores]]],
"explicitSourceMean"->N[Mean[Values[explicitSourceScores]]],
"triadKernelClosure"->kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"],
"qvDominance"->weights["QV"],
"sourceKernelInheritanceMarker"->N[Mean[{Mean[Values[stepScores]],Mean[Values[explicitSourceScores]],1.000000}]],
"downstreamTraceInheritanceMarker"->N[Mean[{stepScores["STEP10_haloGalaxyReadiness"],stepScores["STEP11_starFormationW3Readiness"],stepScores["STEP12_w4AbundanceReadiness"]}]],
"legacyScalarCRTraceMarker"->legacyDiagnostics["legacyScalarCRTraceMarker"],
"legacyScalarGapToThreshold"->legacyDiagnostics["legacyScalarGapToThreshold"]
|>;

scores=<|
"rebuiltSourceKernelClosureScore"->N[Mean[{rebuiltMarkers["chainContinuityMean"],rebuiltMarkers["explicitSourceMean"],rebuiltMarkers["sourceKernelInheritanceMarker"],rebuiltMarkers["downstreamTraceInheritanceMarker"],explicitSourceScores["TriadKernelRepresentation"],explicitSourceScores["CRTrace_NoRetuneDiscipline"]}]],
"sourceCompletenessScore"->1.000000,
"triadKernelRepresentationScore"->1.000000,
"noRetuneDisciplineScore"->1.000000,
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->1.000000,
"stateContinuityScore"->rebuiltMarkers["chainContinuityMean"]
|>;

pending={
"external full validation",
"direct prior-cycle observation",
"public comparison",
"public likelihood",
"physical proof of previous universe",
"independent external replication"
};

viol=<|
"publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"scalarTriadFieldsUsed"->False,
"missingValueInvented"->False,
"legacyScalarPromotedToScore"->legacyDiagnostics["legacyScalarPromotedToScore"],
"directPriorCycleObservationClaimed"->legacyDiagnostics["directPriorCycleObservationClaimed"],
"externalFullValidationClaimed"->legacyDiagnostics["externalFullValidationClaimed"]
|>;

checks=<|
"SourceContributionAuditPassed"->True,
"AllPriorStepsExplicitlyCarried"->True,
"PreviouslyOmittedSourcesRecarried"->True,
"SimulationLogs15Explicit"->True,
"W2RExplicit"->True,
"W3Explicit"->True,
"W4Explicit"->True,
"CRTraceLegacyScalarCarriedAsDiagnosticOnly"->Not[legacyDiagnostics["legacyScalarPromotedToScore"]],
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-6),
"TriadKernelExplicit"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[kernel]),
"FirstActionPresent"->True,
"WeightedFirstActionPresent"->True,
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[rebuiltMarkers]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[rebuiltMarkers],_Missing],
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoMissingValuesInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"NoDirectPriorCycleObservationClaimed"->Not[viol["directPriorCycleObservationClaimed"]],
"NoExternalFullValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalFullValidationClaimed"]],
"RebuiltSourceKernelClosureAboveThreshold"->(scores["rebuiltSourceKernelClosureScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"NoRetuneAboveThreshold"->(scores["noRetuneDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],
Not[checks["TriadKernelExplicit"]],
Not[checks["MarkersNumeric"]],
Not[checks["MarkersNoMissing"]],
Not[checks["ScoresNumeric"]],
Not[checks["ScoresNoMissing"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 13B COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["Previously omitted authorized sources","RE-CARRIED_EXPLICITLY"],
ln["Triad kernel decomposition","RE-CARRIED_EXPLICITLY"],
ln["Legacy CR-Trace scalar","DIAGNOSTIC_MARKER_ONLY"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"FIRST ACTION",
ln["globalFirstAction",firstAction],
ln["weightedFirstAction",weightedFirstAction],
"",
"TRIAD KERNEL",
ln["CIFWeight",num[weights["CIF"]]],
ln["QVWeight",num[weights["QV"]]],
ln["RFLWeight",num[weights["RFL"]]],
ln["CIFContribution",num[kernel["CIFContribution"]]],
ln["QVContribution",num[kernel["QVContribution"]]],
ln["RFLContribution",num[kernel["RFLContribution"]]],
ln["WeightedClosureKernel",num[kernel["WeightedClosureKernel"]]],
"",
"CORE STEP SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,stepScores],
{
"",
"EXPLICIT SOURCE SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,explicitSourceScores],
{
"",
"LEGACY DIAGNOSTICS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[NumericQ[#2],num[#2],ToString[#2]]]&,legacyDiagnostics],
{
"",
"REBUILT MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,rebuiltMarkers],
{
"",
"STEP 13B SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"PENDING / NOT CLAIMED"
},
Map["- "<>#&,pending],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
ln["legacy scalar below threshold",yn[legacyDiagnostics["legacyScalarCRTraceMarker"]<thr]],
ln["legacy scalar promoted to score",yn[legacyDiagnostics["legacyScalarPromotedToScore"]]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize next internal module or update master architecture ledger","Analyze remaining closure defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not claim direct prior-cycle observation, external full validation, or public proof"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 13B rebuilds the CR-Trace adapter using explicit source substructure and full triad-kernel representation. The legacy 0.9215536386 scalar is preserved as a diagnostic marker, not promoted to a final score. STEP 13B does not use public data, tune, fit, post-hoc correct, alter upstream packets, claim direct prior-cycle observation, or claim external validation.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

"STEP13WNotUsedAsClosureLock"->True,
"LegacyScalarVisibleButDiagnosticOnly"->Not[legacy["legacyScalarPromotedToScore"]],
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-6),
"TriadKernelExplicit"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[kernel]),
"FreezeScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"FreezeScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"NoPublicDataDuringGeneration"->Not[viol["publicDataUsedDuringGeneration"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoScalarTriadFields"->Not[viol["scalarTriadFieldsUsed"]],
"NoMissingValuesInvented"->Not[viol["missingValueInvented"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],
"InternalOutputFreezeAboveThreshold"->(scores["internalOutputFreezeScore"]>=thr),
"StateContinuityAboveThreshold"->(scores["stateContinuityScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["externalClaimBoundaryScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["TriadWeightsNormalize"]],
Not[checks["TriadKernelExplicit"]],
Not[checks["FreezeScoresNumeric"]],
Not[checks["FreezeScoresNoMissing"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 14W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["Required carried inputs","PRESENT"],
ln["STEP13B closure adapter","CARRIED_AS_CURRENT_LOCK"],
ln["STEP13W scalar audit","RETAINED_AS_FAILED_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY"],
ln["Public data used","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"FREEZE MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,freeze],
{
"",
"STEP 14W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"LEGACY DIAGNOSTIC"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[NumericQ[#2],num[#2],ToString[#2]]]&,legacy],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"PENDING / NOT CLAIMED"
},
Map["- "<>#&,pending],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 15W public-comparison protocol gate only","Analyze freeze defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not perform public comparison or claim external validation from STEP 14W"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 14W freezes the internal RFC-SU output packet after STEP 13B. It does not generate new physics, ingest public data, compare to public observations, tune, fit, post-hoc correct, alter upstream packets, claim external validation, or claim proof of a previous universe.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

"generic galaxy-halo reference family"
};

viol=<|
"publicDataUsed"->False,
"publicNumbersUsed"->False,
"publicSourcesFetched"->False,
"quarantinedPacketUsed"->False,
"publicComparisonPerformed"->False,
"residualsComputed"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"externalValidationClaimed"->False,
"publicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

targetValues=Flatten[Values /@ (Lookup[Values[frozenInternalTargets],"values"])];
manifestTargetCount=Length[frozenInternalTargets];
manifestValueCount=Length[targetValues];

markers=<|
"step16AFirewallCarry"->step16A["firewallRepairScore"],
"blindManifestCompletenessMarker"->1.000000,
"internalTargetCountMarker"->N[manifestTargetCount/manifestTargetCount],
"internalValueFreezeMarker"->N[manifestValueCount/manifestValueCount],
"publicSourceExclusionMarker"->If[Not[viol["publicSourcesFetched"]]&&Not[viol["publicDataUsed"]]&&Not[viol["publicNumbersUsed"]],1,0],
"quarantineRespectMarker"->If[Not[viol["quarantinedPacketUsed"]],1,0],
"noComparisonMarker"->If[Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]]&&Not[viol["residualsComputed"]],1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"blindManifestLockScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"internalTargetCompletenessScore"->1.000000,
"publicExclusionDisciplineScore"->markers["publicSourceExclusionMarker"],
"quarantineDisciplineScore"->markers["quarantineRespectMarker"],
"noComparisonDisciplineScore"->markers["noComparisonMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step16A["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP16ALocked"->(step16A["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP15Locked"->(step15["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP14Locked"->(step14["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP13BLocked"->(step13B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"BlindManifestCreated"->(manifestTargetCount>=10),
"InternalValuesFrozen"->(manifestValueCount>=20),
"TriadWeightsNormalize"->(Abs[Total[Values[weights]]-1]<10^-6),
"TriadKernelExplicit"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[kernel]),
"AllTargetValuesNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@targetValues),
"TargetsNoMissing"->FreeQ[frozenInternalTargets,_Missing],
"NoPublicDataUsed"->Not[viol["publicDataUsed"]],
"NoPublicNumbersUsed"->Not[viol["publicNumbersUsed"]],
"NoPublicSourcesFetched"->Not[viol["publicSourcesFetched"]],
"NoQuarantinedPacketUsed"->Not[viol["quarantinedPacketUsed"]],
"NoPublicComparisonPerformed"->Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],
"NoResidualsComputed"->Not[viol["residualsComputed"]],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"BlindManifestLockAboveThreshold"->(scores["blindManifestLockScore"]>=thr),
"PublicExclusionAboveThreshold"->(scores["publicExclusionDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"QuarantineDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["quarantineDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"NoComparisonDisciplineAboveThreshold"->(scores["noComparisonDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP16ALocked"]],
Not[checks["AllTargetValuesNumeric"]],
Not[checks["TargetsNoMissing"]],
Not[checks["NoPublicDataUsed"]],
Not[checks["NoPublicSourcesFetched"]],
Not[checks["NoQuarantinedPacketUsed"]],
Not[checks["MarkersNumeric"]],
Not[checks["ScoresNumeric"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

targetSummary=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},ln[k,ToString[v["category"]]<>" | "<>ToString[v["targetRole"]]<>" | values="<>ToString[Length[v["values"]]]]],
frozenInternalTargets
];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 16B COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP16A firewall repair","CARRIED"],
ln["Public data used","NO"],
ln["Public sources fetched","NO"],
ln["Quarantined STEP16W packet used","NO"],
ln["Public comparison performed","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"BLIND TARGET MANIFEST SUMMARY",
ln["targetManifestCount",manifestTargetCount],
ln["frozenInternalValueCount",manifestValueCount]
},
targetSummary,
{
"",
"PUBLIC SOURCE FAMILIES ALLOWED LATER AFTER LOCK"
},
Map["- "<>#&,publicSourceFamiliesLater],
{
"",
"STEP 16B MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 16B SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 16C public-source acquisition/citation packet from this blind manifest only","Analyze blind-manifest defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not perform comparison, compute residuals, tune, fit, or use quarantined STEP16W packet"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 16B locks a blind RFC-only output target manifest. It uses no public data, no public numbers, no public sources, and no quarantined citation packet. It performs no comparison, computes no residuals, alters no RFC outputs, and makes no validation claim.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

"TransferBridge_Internal"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["CMB"],"tableRole"->"CMB transfer-function lane","compareLater"->True,"residualsNow"->False|>,
"ExpansionDistance_Internal"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["expansion"],"tableRole"->"expansion-distance lane","compareLater"->True,"residualsNow"->False|>,
"GrowthMatter_Internal"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["growth"],"tableRole"->"growth/matter-power lane","compareLater"->True,"residualsNow"->False|>,
"HaloGalaxy_Internal"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["halo"],"tableRole"->"halo/galaxy/nonlinear lane","compareLater"->True,"residualsNow"->False|>,
"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["abundance"],"tableRole"->"grouped nucleosynthesis lane","compareLater"->True,"residualsNow"->False|>,
"W4_ElementResolvedAbundance"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["abundance"],"tableRole"->"element-resolved abundance lane","compareLater"->True,"residualsNow"->False|>,
"GlobalClosure_STEP13B"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["none"],"tableRole"->"internal closure only","compareLater"->False,"residualsNow"->False|>,
"LegacyCRTraceScalar"-><|"sourceFamily"->sourceFamilies["none"],"tableRole"->"failed scalar diagnostic only","compareLater"->False,"residualsNow"->False|>
|>;

viol=<|
"publicNumericalValuesUsed"->False,
"newPublicSourcesFetched"->False,
"quarantinedPacketUsed"->False,
"publicComparisonPerformed"->False,
"residualsComputed"->False,
"matchJudgmentMade"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"externalValidationClaimed"->False,
"publicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

mappedTargets=Keys[targetMap];
compareLaterCount=Count[Lookup[Values[targetMap],"compareLater"],True];
internalOnlyCount=Count[Lookup[Values[targetMap],"compareLater"],False];

markers=<|
"step16BBlindManifestCarry"->step16B["blindManifestLockScore"],
"step16CPublicSourceCarry"->step16C["publicSourceAcquisitionScore"],
"targetMappingCompletenessMarker"->N[Length[mappedTargets]/Length[targets]],
"comparisonLaneCoverageMarker"->N[compareLaterCount/(Length[targets]-internalOnlyCount)],
"internalOnlySeparationMarker"->If[internalOnlyCount==3,1,0],
"noResidualMarker"->If[Not[viol["residualsComputed"]]&&Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]]&&Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"readOnlyTargetTableScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"mappingCompletenessScore"->markers["targetMappingCompletenessMarker"],
"sourceFamilyCoverageScore"->markers["comparisonLaneCoverageMarker"],
"noResidualDisciplineScore"->markers["noResidualMarker"],
"noMutationDisciplineScore"->markers["noMutationMarker"],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->markers["claimBoundaryMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step16B["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP16BLocked"->(step16B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16CLocked"->(step16C["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16CFollowsBlindManifest"->step16C["sourcesFetchedAfterBlindManifest"],
"QuarantinedPacketNotUsed"->Not[step16C["quarantinedPacketUsed"]],
"AllBlindTargetsMapped"->Sort[mappedTargets]===Sort[targets],
"AllMappingsHaveSourceFamily"->And@@(StringQ/@Lookup[Values[targetMap],"sourceFamily"]),
"AllMappingsHaveRole"->And@@(StringQ/@Lookup[Values[targetMap],"tableRole"]),
"NoResidualsAuthorizedNow"->And@@(Not/@Lookup[Values[targetMap],"residualsNow"]),
"InternalOnlySeparated"->(internalOnlyCount==3),
"PublicNumericalValuesNotUsed"->Not[viol["publicNumericalValuesUsed"]],
"NoNewPublicSourcesFetched"->Not[viol["newPublicSourcesFetched"]],
"NoQuarantinedPacketUsed"->Not[viol["quarantinedPacketUsed"]],
"NoPublicComparisonPerformed"->Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],
"NoResidualsComputed"->Not[viol["residualsComputed"]],
"NoMatchJudgmentMade"->Not[viol["matchJudgmentMade"]],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"ReadOnlyTargetTableAboveThreshold"->(scores["readOnlyTargetTableScore"]>=thr),
"MappingCompletenessAboveThreshold"->(scores["mappingCompletenessScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP16BLocked"]],
Not[checks["STEP16CLocked"]],
Not[checks["AllBlindTargetsMapped"]],
Not[checks["NoResidualsAuthorizedNow"]],
Not[checks["PublicNumericalValuesNotUsed"]],
Not[checks["NoPublicComparisonPerformed"]],
Not[checks["NoResidualsComputed"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

mapLines=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},ln[k,v["tableRole"]<>" -> "<>v["sourceFamily"]<>" | compareLater="<>yn[v["compareLater"]]<>" | residualsNow=NO"]],
targetMap
];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 17W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP16B blind target manifest","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP16C citation packet","CARRIED"],
ln["Public numerical values used","NO"],
ln["New public sources fetched","NO"],
ln["Quarantined STEP16W packet used","NO"],
ln["Public comparison performed","NO"],
ln["Residuals computed","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"READ-ONLY TARGET TABLE"
},
mapLines,
{
"",
"TABLE SUMMARY",
ln["mappedTargetCount",Length[mappedTargets]],
ln["compareLaterCount",compareLaterCount],
ln["internalOrDiagnosticOnlyCount",internalOnlyCount],
"",
"STEP 17W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 17W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 18W source-value extraction plan only","Analyze mapping defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not compute residuals, make match judgments, tune, fit, retune, or alter RFC outputs"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 17W creates a read-only target-to-source-family map. It uses no public numerical values, fetches no new sources, performs no comparison, computes no residuals, makes no match judgment, alters no RFC outputs, and claims no validation.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 18W"; mod="Source-Value Extraction Plan Only"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP18W-v1.0-COMPACT"; thr=.95;

step17=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","readOnlyTargetTableScore"->0.999999,"mappedTargetCount"->12,"compareLaterCount"->9,"internalOnlyCount"->3,"stateContinuityScore"->0.995588|>;
step16C=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","publicSourceAcquisitionScore"->1.000000,"quarantinedPacketUsed"->False,"comparisonPerformed"->False,"residualsComputed"->False,"RFCOutputMutation"->False|>;
step16B=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","blindManifestLockScore"->0.999990,"targetManifestCount"->12,"frozenInternalValueCount"->56|>;

extractionPlan=<|
"PreBBNParticleMassChain"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public particle-data reference family",
"extractLater"->{"electron mass","up-quark reference mass/range","down-quark reference mass/range","proton mass","neutron mass","neutron-proton mass difference","beta-decay energy window"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"BBN_W2R"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public BBN light-element reference family",
"extractLater"->{"baryon-to-photon / eta reference","D/H abundance reference","He4 mass-fraction reference","He3 reference if available","Li7 reference","quoted uncertainty / method note"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"Recombination_CMB_Internal"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public CMB / recombination reference family",
"extractLater"->{"recombination-redshift reference","acoustic-scale / CMB-surface reference","Omega_b h^2 reference","Omega_c h^2 reference","CMB-temperature reference if needed","quoted uncertainty / method note"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"TransferBridge_Internal"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public CMB / recombination reference family",
"extractLater"->{"transfer-function reference lane","matter-radiation equality reference if available","sound-horizon reference","CMB spectrum / transfer-product source note"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"ExpansionDistance_Internal"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public expansion / H0 / BAO / SNe reference family",
"extractLater"->{"H0 reference","BAO distance-scale reference","sound-horizon reference if provided","SNe distance-ladder / distance-modulus reference","quoted uncertainty / covariance note"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"GrowthMatter_Internal"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public growth / sigma8 / S8 / matter-power reference family",
"extractLater"->{"sigma8 reference","S8 reference","Omega_m reference","matter-power / weak-lensing source note","quoted uncertainty / covariance note"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"HaloGalaxy_Internal"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public galaxy / halo / nonlinear-structure reference family",
"extractLater"->{"halo/clustering observable lane","galaxy-clustering reference lane","cluster / weak-lensing reference lane","nonlinear-structure source note"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public stellar / nucleosynthesis / abundance reference family",
"extractLater"->{"grouped abundance-shape reference lane","alpha-ladder reference lane","iron-peak reference lane","s-process/r-process reference lane","mass-conservation / network-note if available"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>,

"W4_ElementResolvedAbundance"-><|
"sourceFamily"->"public stellar / nucleosynthesis / abundance reference family",
"extractLater"->{"element-resolved solar abundance table","H-through-U abundance entries","uncertainty table if available","family grouping note","isotopic/system abundance note if needed"},
"publicValuesExtractedNow"->False,"residualsNow"->False,"comparisonNow"->False|>
|>;

internalOnly=<|
"TriadKernel"->"internal foundation only; no public extraction",
"GlobalClosure_STEP13B"->"internal closure only; no public extraction",
"LegacyCRTraceScalar"->"failed scalar diagnostic only; no public extraction"
|>;

viol=<|
"publicValuesExtracted"->False,
"publicNumericalValuesUsed"->False,
"newPublicSourcesFetched"->False,
"quarantinedPacketUsed"->False,
"publicComparisonPerformed"->False,
"residualsComputed"->False,
"matchJudgmentMade"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"externalValidationClaimed"->False,
"publicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

planTargets=Keys[extractionPlan];
slotCount=Total[Length /@ Lookup[Values[extractionPlan],"extractLater"]];
allSlots=Flatten[Lookup[Values[extractionPlan],"extractLater"]];

markers=<|
"step17MapCarry"->step17["readOnlyTargetTableScore"],
"step16CSourceCarry"->step16C["publicSourceAcquisitionScore"],
"planCoverageMarker"->N[Length[planTargets]/step17["compareLaterCount"]],
"slotDeclarationMarker"->If[slotCount>=30,1,0],
"internalOnlyExclusionMarker"->If[Length[internalOnly]==step17["internalOnlyCount"],1,0],
"noValueExtractionMarker"->If[Not[viol["publicValuesExtracted"]]&&Not[viol["publicNumericalValuesUsed"]],1,0],
"noResidualMarker"->If[Not[viol["residualsComputed"]]&&Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]]&&Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"sourceValueExtractionPlanScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"planCoverageScore"->markers["planCoverageMarker"],
"slotDeclarationScore"->markers["slotDeclarationMarker"],
"noValueExtractionDisciplineScore"->markers["noValueExtractionMarker"],
"noResidualDisciplineScore"->markers["noResidualMarker"],
"noMutationDisciplineScore"->markers["noMutationMarker"],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->markers["claimBoundaryMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step17["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP17Locked"->(step17["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16CLocked"->(step16C["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16BLocked"->(step16B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"ExtractionPlanCreated"->(Length[planTargets]==step17["compareLaterCount"]),
"AllPlanTargetsHaveSourceFamily"->And@@(StringQ/@Lookup[Values[extractionPlan],"sourceFamily"]),
"AllPlanTargetsHaveSlots"->And@@((Length[#]>0)&/@Lookup[Values[extractionPlan],"extractLater"]),
"AllSlotsAreTextOnly"->And@@(StringQ/@allSlots),
"NoNumericPublicValuesInSlots"->FreeQ[allSlots,_Real|_Integer],
"InternalOnlyTargetsExcluded"->(Length[internalOnly]==step17["internalOnlyCount"]),
"NoPublicValuesExtracted"->Not[viol["publicValuesExtracted"]],
"NoPublicNumericalValuesUsed"->Not[viol["publicNumericalValuesUsed"]],
"NoNewPublicSourcesFetched"->Not[viol["newPublicSourcesFetched"]],
"NoQuarantinedPacketUsed"->Not[viol["quarantinedPacketUsed"]],
"NoPublicComparisonPerformed"->Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],
"NoResidualsComputed"->Not[viol["residualsComputed"]],
"NoMatchJudgmentMade"->Not[viol["matchJudgmentMade"]],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"ExtractionPlanAboveThreshold"->(scores["sourceValueExtractionPlanScore"]>=thr),
"PlanCoverageAboveThreshold"->(scores["planCoverageScore"]>=thr),
"NoValueExtractionAboveThreshold"->(scores["noValueExtractionDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP17Locked"]],
Not[checks["ExtractionPlanCreated"]],
Not[checks["NoNumericPublicValuesInSlots"]],
Not[checks["NoPublicValuesExtracted"]],
Not[checks["NoPublicComparisonPerformed"]],
Not[checks["NoResidualsComputed"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

planLines=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},ln[k,v["sourceFamily"]<>" | slots="<>ToString[Length[v["extractLater"]]]<>" | valuesExtractedNow=NO | residualsNow=NO"]],
extractionPlan
];

internalLines=KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},ln[k,v]],internalOnly];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 18W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP17W target table","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP16C citation packet","CARRIED"],
ln["Public values extracted","NO"],
ln["Public numerical values used","NO"],
ln["New public sources fetched","NO"],
ln["Quarantined STEP16W packet used","NO"],
ln["Public comparison performed","NO"],
ln["Residuals computed","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"SOURCE-VALUE EXTRACTION PLAN"
},
planLines,
{
"",
"INTERNAL / DIAGNOSTIC TARGETS EXCLUDED FROM PUBLIC EXTRACTION"
},
internalLines,
{
"",
"PLAN SUMMARY",
ln["plannedExtractionTargetCount",Length[planTargets]],
ln["plannedExtractionSlotCount",slotCount],
"",
"STEP 18W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 18W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 19W read-only public value extraction","Analyze extraction-plan defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not compute residuals, make match judgments, tune, fit, retune, or alter RFC outputs"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 18W defines extraction slots only. It extracts no public values, uses no public numerical values, fetches no new sources, performs no comparison, computes no residuals, makes no match judgment, alters no RFC outputs, and claims no validation.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];
