|>;

viol=<|
"residualsComputed"->False,
"publicComparisonPerformed"->False,
"matchJudgmentMade"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"quarantinedPacketUsed"->False,
"externalValidationClaimed"->False,
"publicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

extractionTargetCount=Length[sourceValues];
numericValueCount=Length[Cases[sourceValues,_Integer|_Real,Infinity]];
statusCount=Count[Cases[sourceValues,_String,Infinity],"EXTRACTED_READ_ONLY"];

markers=<|
"step18PlanCarry"->step18["sourceValueExtractionPlanScore"],
"step17MapCarry"->step17["readOnlyTargetTableScore"],
"step16BBlindManifestCarry"->step16B["blindManifestLockScore"],
"step16CSourceCarry"->step16C["publicSourceAcquisitionScore"],
"readOnlyExtractionMarker"->1.000000,
"targetCoverageMarker"->N[extractionTargetCount/step18["plannedExtractionTargetCount"]],
"numericExtractionMarker"->If[numericValueCount>=40,1,0],
"sourceStatusMarker"->If[statusCount==extractionTargetCount,1,0],
"noResidualMarker"->If[Not[viol["residualsComputed"]]&&Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]]&&Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"readOnlyPublicValueExtractionScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"targetCoverageScore"->markers["targetCoverageMarker"],
"numericExtractionScore"->markers["numericExtractionMarker"],
"noResidualDisciplineScore"->markers["noResidualMarker"],
"noMutationDisciplineScore"->markers["noMutationMarker"],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->markers["claimBoundaryMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step18["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP18Locked"->(step18["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP17Locked"->(step17["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16BLocked"->(step16B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16CLocked"->(step16C["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"QuarantinedPacketNotUsed"->Not[step16C["quarantinedPacketUsed"]],
"ExtractionTargetsCovered"->(extractionTargetCount==step18["plannedExtractionTargetCount"]),
"NumericValuesExtracted"->(numericValueCount>=40),
"SourceStatusPresent"->(statusCount==extractionTargetCount),
"InternalOnlyStillExcluded"->(Length[internalOnly]==3),
"NoResidualsComputed"->Not[viol["residualsComputed"]],
"NoPublicComparisonPerformed"->Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],
"NoMatchJudgmentMade"->Not[viol["matchJudgmentMade"]],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoQuarantinedPacketUsed"->Not[viol["quarantinedPacketUsed"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"ReadOnlyExtractionAboveThreshold"->(scores["readOnlyPublicValueExtractionScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP18Locked"]],
Not[checks["ExtractionTargetsCovered"]],
Not[checks["NoResidualsComputed"]],
Not[checks["NoPublicComparisonPerformed"]],
Not[checks["NoRFCOutputAltered"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

valueLines=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},ln[k,"values="<>ToString[Length[v]]<>" | status="<>ToString[v["sourceStatus"]]<>" | residuals=NO | comparison=NO"]],
sourceValues
];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 19W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP18W extraction plan","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP17W target table","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP16B blind manifest","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP16C citation packet","CARRIED"],
ln["Public values extracted","YES_READ_ONLY"],
ln["Public comparison performed","NO"],
ln["Residuals computed","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"READ-ONLY SOURCE VALUE TABLE SUMMARY"
},
valueLines,
{
"",
"INTERNAL / DIAGNOSTIC TARGETS STILL EXCLUDED"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,#2]&,internalOnly],
{
"",
"EXTRACTION SUMMARY",
ln["extractionTargetCount",extractionTargetCount],
ln["numericPublicValueCount",numericValueCount],
ln["sourceStatusCount",statusCount],
"",
"STEP 19W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 19W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 20W residual-audit protocol only","Analyze extraction defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not compute residuals, make match judgments, tune, fit, retune, or alter RFC outputs"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 19W extracts public values into a read-only reference table after the blind RFC manifest. It performs no comparison, computes no residuals, makes no match judgment, alters no RFC outputs, tunes nothing, fits nothing, retunes nothing, and claims no validation.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 20W"; mod="Residual-Audit Protocol Only"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP20W-v1.0-COMPACT"; thr=.95;

step19=<|
"status"->"PASS_LOCKED",
"readOnlyPublicValueExtractionScore"->0.999999,
"numericPublicValueCount"->93,
"extractionTargetCount"->9,
"stateContinuityScore"->0.995588
|>;

step18=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","sourceValueExtractionPlanScore"->1.000000|>;
step17=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","readOnlyTargetTableScore"->0.999999|>;
step16B=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","blindManifestLockScore"->0.999990|>;

residualLanes=<|
"PreBBNParticleMassChain"-><|"laneType"->"particle_mass_handoff","methodLater"->"dimensionless normalized mass/window residuals","uncertaintyRule"->"use quoted uncertainty/range where available","residualsNow"->False|>,
"BBN_W2R"-><|"laneType"->"BBN_light_element_proxy","methodLater"->"dimensionless abundance/eta residuals","uncertaintyRule"->"use quoted observational/theory uncertainty; no tuning","residualsNow"->False|>,
"Recombination_CMB_Internal"-><|"laneType"->"CMB_recombination_surface","methodLater"->"parameter residuals only where RFC target is comparable","uncertaintyRule"->"use public quoted uncertainty/covariance note","residualsNow"->False|>,
"TransferBridge_Internal"-><|"laneType"->"CMB_transfer_bridge","methodLater"->"bridge-readiness versus source-product availability only unless direct numeric target exists","uncertaintyRule"->"no forced residual if observable mismatch","residualsNow"->False|>,
"ExpansionDistance_Internal"-><|"laneType"->"expansion_distance","methodLater"->"H0/BAO/SNe residuals only for predeclared targets","uncertaintyRule"->"keep source families separated; no averaging to fit","residualsNow"->False|>,
"GrowthMatter_Internal"-><|"laneType"->"growth_matter","methodLater"->"sigma8/S8/OmegaM residuals for frozen internal proxies","uncertaintyRule"->"use source-specific uncertainty; preserve tensions","residualsNow"->False|>,
"HaloGalaxy_Internal"-><|"laneType"->"halo_galaxy_nonlinear","methodLater"->"diagnostic residuals only where comparable observable exists","uncertaintyRule"->"no invented mapping; non-comparable slots marked pending","residualsNow"->False|>,
"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis"-><|"laneType"->"grouped_nucleosynthesis","methodLater"->"shape/group residuals against abundance families","uncertaintyRule"->"group definitions frozen before calculation","residualsNow"->False|>,
"W4_ElementResolvedAbundance"-><|"laneType"->"element_resolved_abundance","methodLater"->"elementwise/log-abundance residuals using frozen element set","uncertaintyRule"->"do not remove outliers after seeing result","residualsNow"->False|>
|>;

protocolRules=<|
"ResidualsOnlyAfterProtocolLock"->True,
"RFCOutputsRemainFrozen"->True,
"PublicValuesReadOnly"->True,
"NoRetuneFromResiduals"->True,
"NoCurveFitFromResiduals"->True,
"NoPostHocCorrectionFromResiduals"->True,
"NoUpstreamPacketAlteration"->True,
"NoObservableCherryPicking"->True,
"NoAveragingAcrossTensionedSourcesToImproveFit"->True,
"NonComparableSlotsMustRemainPending"->True,
"UncertaintyMustBeSourceSpecific"->True,
"AllResidualScoresBelowThresholdAreFailAnalysis"->True,
"FailDoesNotAuthorizeRetune"->True,
"ResidualScoreIsAuditOutputNotValidationClaim"->True,
"ExternalValidationRequiresSeparateFuture Step"->True
|>;

scorePolicy=<|
"passThreshold"->thr,
"laneResidualBelowThresholdVerdict"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
"globalResidualBelowThresholdVerdict"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
"allowedFailureAction"->"analyze source/claim/mapping boundary only",
"forbiddenFailureAction"->"retune, fit, post-hoc correct, remove target, alter upstream packet"
|>;

viol=<|
"residualsComputed"->False,
"publicComparisonPerformed"->False,
"matchJudgmentMade"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"upstreamPacketAltered"->False,
"targetRemovedAfterPublicValues"->False,
"sourceAveragedToImproveFit"->False,
"externalValidationClaimed"->False,
"publicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

laneCount=Length[residualLanes];
ruleCount=Length[protocolRules];

markers=<|
"step19ReadOnlyValueCarry"->step19["readOnlyPublicValueExtractionScore"],
"step18PlanCarry"->step18["sourceValueExtractionPlanScore"],
"step17MapCarry"->step17["readOnlyTargetTableScore"],
"step16BBlindManifestCarry"->step16B["blindManifestLockScore"],
"laneCoverageMarker"->N[laneCount/step19["extractionTargetCount"]],
"protocolRuleCoverageMarker"->If[ruleCount>=12,1,0],
"noResidualMarker"->If[Not[viol["residualsComputed"]]&&Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamPacketAltered"]],1,0],
"noCherryPickMarker"->If[Not[viol["targetRemovedAfterPublicValues"]]&&Not[viol["sourceAveragedToImproveFit"]],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]]&&Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"residualAuditProtocolScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"laneCoverageScore"->markers["laneCoverageMarker"],
"protocolRuleCoverageScore"->markers["protocolRuleCoverageMarker"],
"noResidualDisciplineScore"->markers["noResidualMarker"],
"noMutationDisciplineScore"->markers["noMutationMarker"],
"noCherryPickDisciplineScore"->markers["noCherryPickMarker"],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->markers["claimBoundaryMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step19["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP19Locked"->(step19["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP18Locked"->(step18["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP17Locked"->(step17["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16BLocked"->(step16B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"ResidualLanesCreated"->(laneCount==step19["extractionTargetCount"]),
"AllLanesHaveMethod"->And@@(StringQ/@Lookup[Values[residualLanes],"methodLater"]),
"AllLanesHaveUncertaintyRule"->And@@(StringQ/@Lookup[Values[residualLanes],"uncertaintyRule"]),
"NoLaneResidualsNow"->And@@(Not/@Lookup[Values[residualLanes],"residualsNow"]),
"ProtocolRulesLocked"->And@@Values[protocolRules],
"BelowThresholdMeansFailAnalysis"->(scorePolicy["laneResidualBelowThresholdVerdict"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"FailDoesNotAuthorizeRetune"->protocolRules["FailDoesNotAuthorizeRetune"],
"ResidualScoreNotValidationClaim"->protocolRules["ResidualScoreIsAuditOutputNotValidationClaim"],
"NoResidualsComputed"->Not[viol["residualsComputed"]],
"NoPublicComparisonPerformed"->Not[viol["publicComparisonPerformed"]],
"NoMatchJudgmentMade"->Not[viol["matchJudgmentMade"]],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamPacketAltered"->Not[viol["upstreamPacketAltered"]],
"NoTargetRemovedAfterPublicValues"->Not[viol["targetRemovedAfterPublicValues"]],
"NoSourceAveragedToImproveFit"->Not[viol["sourceAveragedToImproveFit"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"ResidualAuditProtocolAboveThreshold"->(scores["residualAuditProtocolScore"]>=thr),
"LaneCoverageAboveThreshold"->(scores["laneCoverageScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP19Locked"]],
Not[checks["ResidualLanesCreated"]],
Not[checks["NoLaneResidualsNow"]],
Not[checks["ProtocolRulesLocked"]],
Not[checks["NoResidualsComputed"]],
Not[checks["NoPublicComparisonPerformed"]],
Not[checks["NoRFCOutputAltered"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

score=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[score>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

laneLines=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},ln[k,v["laneType"]<>" | methodLater="<>v["methodLater"]<>" | residualsNow=NO"]],
residualLanes
];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 20W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP19W read-only public values","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP18W extraction plan","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP17W target map","CARRIED"],
ln["Residuals computed","NO"],
ln["Public comparison performed","NO"],
ln["Match judgment made","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"RESIDUAL-AUDIT LANES"
},
laneLines,
{
"",
"PROTOCOL RULES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,yn[#2]]&,protocolRules],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,#2]&,scorePolicy],
{
"",
"STEP 20W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 20W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"STATUS",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[score]],
ln["automatic violation detected",yn[autoViolation]],
ln["outputLockStatus",status],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 21W residual calculation lane by lane","Analyze residual-protocol defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not tune, fit, retune, alter RFC outputs, remove targets, average sources to improve fit, or claim validation"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 20W locks the residual-audit protocol only. It computes no residuals, performs no comparison, makes no match judgment, alters no RFC outputs, tunes nothing, fits nothing, retunes nothing, removes no targets, averages no sources to improve fit, and claims no validation.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",status]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 20W COMPACT CERTIFICATE
========================================
RUN: STEP 20W
MODULE: Residual-Audit Protocol Only
VERSION: RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP20W-v1.0-COMPACT

SOURCE GATE
STEP19W read-only public values: CARRIED
STEP18W extraction plan: CARRIED
STEP17W target map: CARRIED
Residuals computed: NO
Public comparison performed: NO
Match judgment made: NO
Audit verdict: PASS

RESIDUAL-AUDIT LANES
PreBBNParticleMassChain: particle_mass_handoff | methodLater=dimensionless normalized mass/window residuals | residualsNow=NO
BBN_W2R: BBN_light_element_proxy | methodLater=dimensionless abundance/eta residuals | residualsNow=NO
Recombination_CMB_Internal: CMB_recombination_surface | methodLater=parameter residuals only where RFC target is comparable | residualsNow=NO
TransferBridge_Internal: CMB_transfer_bridge | methodLater=bridge-readiness versus source-product availability only unless direct numeric target exists | residualsNow=NO
ExpansionDistance_Internal: expansion_distance | methodLater=H0/BAO/SNe residuals only for predeclared targets | residualsNow=NO
GrowthMatter_Internal: growth_matter | methodLater=sigma8/S8/OmegaM residuals for frozen internal proxies | residualsNow=NO
HaloGalaxy_Internal: halo_galaxy_nonlinear | methodLater=diagnostic residuals only where comparable observable exists | residualsNow=NO
W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis: grouped_nucleosynthesis | methodLater=shape/group residuals against abundance families | residualsNow=NO
W4_ElementResolvedAbundance: element_resolved_abundance | methodLater=elementwise/log-abundance residuals using frozen element set | residualsNow=NO

PROTOCOL RULES
ResidualsOnlyAfterProtocolLock: YES
RFCOutputsRemainFrozen: YES
PublicValuesReadOnly: YES
NoRetuneFromResiduals: YES
NoCurveFitFromResiduals: YES
NoPostHocCorrectionFromResiduals: YES
NoUpstreamPacketAlteration: YES
NoObservableCherryPicking: YES
NoAveragingAcrossTensionedSourcesToImproveFit: YES
NonComparableSlotsMustRemainPending: YES
UncertaintyMustBeSourceSpecific: YES
AllResidualScoresBelowThresholdAreFailAnalysis: YES
FailDoesNotAuthorizeRetune: YES
ResidualScoreIsAuditOutputNotValidationClaim: YES
ExternalValidationRequiresSeparateFuture Step: YES

SCORE POLICY
passThreshold: 0.95
laneResidualBelowThresholdVerdict: FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS
globalResidualBelowThresholdVerdict: FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS
allowedFailureAction: analyze source/claim/mapping boundary only
forbiddenFailureAction: retune, fit, post-hoc correct, remove target, alter upstream packet

STEP 20W MARKERS
step19ReadOnlyValueCarry: 0.999999
step18PlanCarry: 1.000000
step17MapCarry: 0.999999
step16BBlindManifestCarry: 0.999990
laneCoverageMarker: 1.000000
protocolRuleCoverageMarker: 1.000000
noResidualMarker: 1.000000
noMutationMarker: 1.000000
noCherryPickMarker: 1.000000
claimBoundaryMarker: 1.000000

STEP 20W SCORES
residualAuditProtocolScore: 0.999999
laneCoverageScore: 1.000000
protocolRuleCoverageScore: 1.000000
noResidualDisciplineScore: 1.000000
noMutationDisciplineScore: 1.000000
noCherryPickDisciplineScore: 1.000000
claimBoundaryDisciplineScore: 1.000000
stateContinuityScore: 0.995588

CHECKS
STEP19Locked: PASS
STEP18Locked: PASS
STEP17Locked: PASS
STEP16BLocked: PASS
ResidualLanesCreated: PASS
AllLanesHaveMethod: PASS
AllLanesHaveUncertaintyRule: PASS
NoLaneResidualsNow: PASS
ProtocolRulesLocked: PASS
BelowThresholdMeansFailAnalysis: PASS
FailDoesNotAuthorizeRetune: PASS
ResidualScoreNotValidationClaim: PASS
NoResidualsComputed: PASS
NoPublicComparisonPerformed: PASS
NoMatchJudgmentMade: PASS
NoRFCOutputAltered: PASS
NoParameterSearch: PASS
NoCurveFit: PASS
NoPostHocCorrection: PASS
NoUpstreamRetune: PASS
NoUpstreamPacketAltered: PASS
NoTargetRemovedAfterPublicValues: PASS
NoSourceAveragedToImproveFit: PASS
NoExternalValidationClaimed: PASS
NoPublicProofClaimed: PASS
MarkersNumeric: PASS
ScoresNumeric: PASS
MarkersNoMissing: PASS
ScoresNoMissing: PASS
ResidualAuditProtocolAboveThreshold: PASS
LaneCoverageAboveThreshold: PASS
ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold: PASS

STATUS
Pass threshold: 0.950000
compact check score: 1.000000
automatic violation detected: NO
outputLockStatus: PASS_LOCKED
allowedNextUse: Initialize STEP 21W residual calculation lane by lane
forbiddenNextUse: Do not tune, fit, retune, alter RFC outputs, remove targets, average sources to improve fit, or claim validation

CLAIM BOUNDARY
STEP 20W locks the residual-audit protocol only. It computes no residuals, performs no comparison, makes no match judgment, alters no RFC outputs, tunes nothing, fits nothing, retunes nothing, removes no targets, averages no sources to improve fit, and claims no validation.

FINAL STATUS
LOCKED AS: PASS_LOCKED

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 21W"; mod="Residual Calculation, Lane by Lane"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP21W-v1.0-COMPACT"; thr=.95;

step20=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","residualAuditProtocolScore"->0.999999,"laneCoverageScore"->1.000000,"stateContinuityScore"->0.995588|>;
step19=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","readOnlyPublicValueExtractionScore"->0.999999,"numericPublicValueCount"->93|>;
step18=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","sourceValueExtractionPlanScore"->1.000000|>;
step17=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","readOnlyTargetTableScore"->0.999999|>;
step16B=<|"status"->"PASS_LOCKED","blindManifestLockScore"->0.999990|>;

rfc=<|
"Particle"-><|"electron"->0.511001,"up"->2.143051,"down"->4.553628,"proton"->937.654436,"neutron"->938.981176,"npSplit"->1.326740,"betaWindow"->0.815739|>,
"BBN"-><|"eta10Proxy"->6.068,"etaBRFC"->6.100*10^-10,"triadicLi7ObservedRatio"->0.999643,"maxLightElementDriftPct"->0.801015,"bbnPersistenceScore"->0.997207|>,
"Expansion"-><|"H0Proxy"->70.173000,"hubbleProxyRatio"->0.958858,"expansionPersistenceScore"->0.984349|>,
"Growth"-><|"sigma8ProxyInternal"->0.808000,"S8ProxyInternal"->0.808000,"primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"->0.993175|>,
"W4"-><|"shapeCorrelation"->0.9946143083,"w4ElementAbundanceAdapterReadinessScore"->0.995047|>
|>;

pub=<|
"Particle"-><|"electron"->0.51099895069,"up"->2.16,"down"->4.70,"proton"->938.27208943,"neutron"->939.56542194,"npSplit"->1.29333251,"betaWindow"->0.78233356|>,
"BBN"-><|"eta10DHe"->6.040,"eta10CMBCombined"->6.115,"D_H_times1e6"->25.47,"Yp"->0.245,"Li7_H"->1.6*10^-10|>,
"CMB"-><|"omegaBh2"->0.0224,"omegaCh2"->0.120,"H0"->67.4,"OmegaM"->0.315,"sigma8"->0.811|>,
"Expansion"-><|"H0Planck"->67.4,"H0PantheonSH0ES"->73.5,"OmegaMPantheon"->0.334,"DESI_Lya_DH_over_rd"->8.632,"DESI_Lya_DM_over_rd"->38.99|>,
"Growth"-><|"sigma8Planck"->0.811,"S8_DESY3_3x2pt"->0.776,"S8_DESY3_cosmicShear"->0.759,"S8_KiDS1000"->0.759|>,
"Halo"-><|"S8_DESY3_clusters"->0.864,"S8_DESY3_clustersPlus3x2pt"->0.811|>,
"Abundance"-><|"H"->12.00,"He"->10.914,"Li"->0.96,"C"->8.46,"N"->7.83,"O"->8.69,"Fe"->7.46|>
|>;

scoreClose[p_,o_]:=N[Clip[1-Abs[N[p]-N[o]]/Max[Abs[N[p]],Abs[N[o]],10^-30],{0,1}]];
comp[name_,p_,o_]:=<|"component"->name,"rfc"->N[p],"public"->N[o],"fracResidual"->N[Abs[N[p]-N[o]]/Max[Abs[N[p]],Abs[N[o]],10^-30]],"score"->scoreClose[p,o]|>;
compScore[name_,s_]:=<|"component"->name,"rfc"->"frozen_internal_score","public"->"audit_slot","fracResidual"->N[1-s],"score"->N[s]|>;
laneFromComps[comps_]:=<|"laneScore"->N[Min[Lookup[comps,"score"]]],"componentCount"->Length[comps],"components"->comps,"status"->""|>;
pendingLane[reason_]:=<|"laneScore"->"PENDING_NONCOMPARABLE","componentCount"->0,"components"->{},"reason"->reason,"status"->"PENDING_NONCOMPARABLE"|>;

lanes=<|
"PreBBNParticleMassChain"->laneFromComps[{
comp["electron_mass",rfc["Particle","electron"],pub["Particle","electron"]],
comp["up_quark_mass",rfc["Particle","up"],pub["Particle","up"]],
comp["down_quark_mass",rfc["Particle","down"],pub["Particle","down"]],
comp["proton_mass",rfc["Particle","proton"],pub["Particle","proton"]],
comp["neutron_mass",rfc["Particle","neutron"],pub["Particle","neutron"]],
comp["neutron_proton_split",rfc["Particle","npSplit"],pub["Particle","npSplit"]],
comp["beta_window",rfc["Particle","betaWindow"],pub["Particle","betaWindow"]]
}],

"BBN_W2R"->laneFromComps[{
comp["eta10_vs_DHe",rfc["BBN","eta10Proxy"],pub["BBN","eta10DHe"]],
comp["eta10_vs_CMB_BBN_combined",rfc["BBN","eta10Proxy"],pub["BBN","eta10CMBCombined"]],
compScore["triadic_Li7_observed_ratio",rfc["BBN","triadicLi7ObservedRatio"]],
compScore["max_light_element_drift_score",1-rfc["BBN","maxLightElementDriftPct"]/100],
compScore["bbn_persistence_score",rfc["BBN","bbnPersistenceScore"]]
}],

"Recombination_CMB_Internal"->pendingLane["readiness markers are not direct public CMB parameters; no forced residual"],
"TransferBridge_Internal"->pendingLane["transfer bridge has no direct numeric transfer-function target in this compact table"],

"ExpansionDistance_Internal"->laneFromComps[{
comp["H0_proxy_vs_Planck",rfc["Expansion","H0Proxy"],pub["Expansion","H0Planck"]],
comp["H0_proxy_vs_Pantheon_SH0ES",rfc["Expansion","H0Proxy"],pub["Expansion","H0PantheonSH0ES"]],
compScore["expansion_persistence_score",rfc["Expansion","expansionPersistenceScore"]]
}],

"GrowthMatter_Internal"->laneFromComps[{
comp["sigma8_proxy_vs_Planck",rfc["Growth","sigma8ProxyInternal"],pub["Growth","sigma8Planck"]],
comp["S8_proxy_vs_DESY3_3x2pt",rfc["Growth","S8ProxyInternal"],pub["Growth","S8_DESY3_3x2pt"]],
comp["S8_proxy_vs_DESY3_cosmic_shear",rfc["Growth","S8ProxyInternal"],pub["Growth","S8_DESY3_cosmicShear"]],
comp["S8_proxy_vs_KiDS1000",rfc["Growth","S8ProxyInternal"],pub["Growth","S8_KiDS1000"]],
compScore["growth_bridge_readiness_score",rfc["Growth","primordialGrowthBridgeReadinessScore"]]
}],

"HaloGalaxy_Internal"->pendingLane["halo/galaxy readiness markers are not direct cluster S8 observables; no invented mapping"],
"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis"->pendingLane["grouped public abundance-shape residual requires frozen grouping table first"],
"W4_ElementResolvedAbundance"->laneFromComps[{
compScore["frozen_W4_shape_correlation",rfc["W4","shapeCorrelation"]],
compScore["frozen_W4_adapter_readiness",rfc["W4","w4ElementAbundanceAdapterReadinessScore"]]
}]
|>;

lanes=AssociationMap[
If[NumericQ[lanes[#]["laneScore"]],
Append[lanes[#],"status"->If[lanes[#]["laneScore"]>=thr,"PASS","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]],
lanes[#]
]&,
Keys[lanes]
];

scoredLanes=Select[lanes,NumericQ[#["laneScore"]]&];
pendingLanes=Select[lanes,Not[NumericQ[#["laneScore"]]]&];
failLanes=Select[scoredLanes,#["laneScore"]<thr&];
passLanes=Select[scoredLanes,#["laneScore"]>=thr&];

scoredLaneScores=Lookup[Values[scoredLanes],"laneScore"];
residualAuditScore=If[Length[scoredLaneScores]>0,N[Min[scoredLaneScores]],0];
residualAuditVerdict=If[Length[failLanes]>0,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS","PASS_LOCKED"];

viol=<|
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"parameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"curveFitPerformed"->False,
"postHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"upstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"upstreamPacketAltered"->False,
"targetRemovedAfterPublicValues"->False,
"sourceAveragedToImproveFit"->False,
"externalValidationClaimed"->False,
"publicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

markers=<|
"step20ProtocolCarry"->step20["residualAuditProtocolScore"],
"step19ReadOnlyValueCarry"->step19["readOnlyPublicValueExtractionScore"],
"laneCalculationCoverageMarker"->N[Length[lanes]/9],
"scoredLaneSeparationMarker"->If[Length[scoredLanes]+Length[pendingLanes]==Length[lanes],1,0],
"failLaneFlaggingMarker"->If[Length[failLanes]>=0,1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]]&&Not[viol["upstreamPacketAltered"]],1,0],
"noFitRetuneMarker"->If[Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]]&&Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]]&&Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]]&&Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"residualAuditExecutionScore"->residualAuditScore,
"protocolExecutionDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"scoredLaneCount"->Length[scoredLanes],
"passLaneCount"->Length[passLanes],
"failLaneCount"->Length[failLanes],
"pendingLaneCount"->Length[pendingLanes],
"stateContinuityScore"->step20["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP20Locked"->(step20["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP19Locked"->(step19["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP18Locked"->(step18["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP17Locked"->(step17["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP16BLocked"->(step16B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"ResidualsComputedForScoredLanes"->(Length[scoredLanes]>0),
"PendingLanesSeparated"->(Length[pendingLanes]>=0),
"FailLanesFlagged"->And@@(Lookup[Values[failLanes],"status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"BelowThresholdMeansFail"->If[Length[failLanes]>0,residualAuditVerdict=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",True],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->Not[viol["RFCOutputAltered"]],
"NoParameterSearch"->Not[viol["parameterSearchPerformed"]],
"NoCurveFit"->Not[viol["curveFitPerformed"]],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->Not[viol["postHocCorrectionPerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->Not[viol["upstreamRetunePerformed"]],
"NoUpstreamPacketAltered"->Not[viol["upstreamPacketAltered"]],
"NoTargetRemovedAfterPublicValues"->Not[viol["targetRemovedAfterPublicValues"]],
"NoSourceAveragedToImproveFit"->Not[viol["sourceAveragedToImproveFit"]],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->Not[viol["externalValidationClaimed"]],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->Not[viol["publicProofClaimed"]],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Select[Values[scores],NumericQ]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"FailureDoesNotAuthorizeRetune"->True,
"ResidualScoreNotValidationClaim"->True
|>;

autoViolation=Or[
Not[checks["STEP20Locked"]],
Not[checks["ResidualsComputedForScoredLanes"]],
Not[checks["FailLanesFlagged"]],
Not[checks["NoRFCOutputAltered"]],
Or@@Values[viol]
];

compactCheckScore=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
outputLockStatus=If[autoViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",residualAuditVerdict];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

laneLines=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},
If[NumericQ[v["laneScore"]],
ln[k,"laneScore="<>num[v["laneScore"]]<>" | status="<>v["status"]<>" | components="<>ToString[v["componentCount"]]],
ln[k,"laneScore=PENDING_NONCOMPARABLE | status=PENDING_NONCOMPARABLE | reason="<>v["reason"]]
]
],
lanes
];

failLines=If[Length[failLanes]==0,{"- none"},KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" score="<>num[v["laneScore"]]],failLanes]];
pendingLines=If[Length[pendingLanes]==0,{"- none"},KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" reason="<>v["reason"]],pendingLanes]];

componentLines=Flatten@KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},
If[NumericQ[v["laneScore"]],
Join[{"["<>k<>"]"},Map["  "<>#["component"]<>" | score="<>num[#["score"]]<>" | fracResidual="<>num[#["fracResidual"]]&,v["components"]]],
{}]
],
lanes
];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 21W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP20W residual protocol","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP19W read-only public values","CARRIED"],
ln["Residuals computed","YES_AUDIT_ONLY"],
ln["RFC outputs altered","NO"],
ln["Tuning / fitting / retune","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PROCEDURAL_PASS_RESIDUAL_RESULT_SEPARATE"],
"",
"LANE RESIDUAL SUMMARY"
},
laneLines,
{
"",
"FAILED LANES BELOW THRESHOLD"
},
failLines,
{
"",
"PENDING NON-COMPARABLE LANES"
},
pendingLines,
{
"",
"COMPONENT SCORES"
},
componentLines,
{
"",
"STEP 21W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[NumericQ[#2],num[#2],#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"STEP 21W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["residualAuditScore",num[residualAuditScore]],
ln["compact check score",num[compactCheckScore]],
"",

ln["outputLockStatus",outputLockStatus],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[outputLockStatus=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 22W residual interpretation boundary","Analyze failed residual lanes only"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not tune, fit, retune, alter RFC outputs, remove targets, average sources to improve fit, or claim validation"],

"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 21W computes residual scores as audit outputs only. Any score below 0.95 is FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS. Failure authorizes analysis only, not retuning, fitting, post-hoc correction, target removal, upstream alteration, source averaging to improve fit, or validation claims.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",

}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 21A"; mod="Failed Residual Lane Analysis / Output-Status Repair"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP21A-v1.0-COMPACT"; thr=.95;

step21W=<|
"status"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
"residualAuditScore"->0.939356,
"protocolExecutionDisciplineScore"->1.000000,
"scoredLaneCount"->5,
"passLaneCount"->4,
"failLaneCount"->1,
"pendingLaneCount"->4,
"stateContinuityScore"->0.995588,
"logicDefect"->"outputLockStatus printed unresolved If expression; FailLanesFlagged check printed FAIL despite lane being visibly flagged"
|>;

growthLane=<|
"laneScore"->0.939356,
"status"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
"components"->{
<|"name"->"sigma8_proxy_vs_Planck","score"->0.996301,"fracResidual"->0.003699,"driver"->False|>,
<|"name"->"S8_proxy_vs_DESY3_3x2pt","score"->0.960396,"fracResidual"->0.039604,"driver"->False|>,
<|"name"->"S8_proxy_vs_DESY3_cosmic_shear","score"->0.939356,"fracResidual"->0.060644,"driver"->True|>,
<|"name"->"S8_proxy_vs_KiDS1000","score"->0.939356,"fracResidual"->0.060644,"driver"->True|>,
<|"name"->"growth_bridge_readiness_score","score"->0.993175,"fracResidual"->0.006825,"driver"->False|>
}
|>;

scoredLaneStatuses=<|
"PreBBNParticleMassChain"->"PASS",
"BBN_W2R"->"PASS",
"ExpansionDistance_Internal"->"PASS",
"GrowthMatter_Internal"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
"W4_ElementResolvedAbundance"->"PASS"
|>;

pendingLanes=<|
"Recombination_CMB_Internal"->"readiness markers are not direct public CMB parameters; no forced residual",
"TransferBridge_Internal"->"transfer bridge has no direct numeric transfer-function target in this compact table",
"HaloGalaxy_Internal"->"halo/galaxy readiness markers are not direct cluster S8 observables; no invented mapping",
"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis"->"grouped public abundance-shape residual requires frozen grouping table first"
|>;

logicRepair=<|
"FailLanesFlaggedRepair"->"Use AllTrue over fail-lane status list, not list==string comparison",
"OutputLockStatusRepair"->"Set outputLockStatus directly to residualAuditVerdict unless procedural violation exists",
"AllowedNextUseRepair"->"If outputLockStatus is FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS, next use is failed-lane analysis only",
"ResidualFailPreserved"->True,
"GrowthMatterFailPreserved"->True,
"Step21WNotPromotedToPass"->True
|>;

viol=<|
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"PublicValueAltered"->False,
"TargetRemoved"->False,
"SourceAveragedToImproveFit"->False,
"ParameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"CurveFitPerformed"->False,
"PostHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"UpstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"UpstreamPacketAltered"->False,
"FailureSoftened"->False,
"ExternalValidationClaimed"->False,
"PublicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

failDrivers=Select[growthLane["components"],#["driver"]===True&];
growthFailCause="S8 weak-lensing tension: RFC S8 proxy stayed at 0.808000 while DESY3 cosmic shear and KiDS1000 slots were 0.759000, giving component score 0.939356 below threshold.";
analysisVerdict="GrowthMatter_Internal remains FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS; failure is localized to S8 weak-lensing components, not sigma8 Planck or internal growth readiness.";

repairedFailLanesFlagged=AllTrue[Values[scoredLaneStatuses],#=="PASS"||#=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"&]&&MemberQ[Values[scoredLaneStatuses],"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"];
residualAuditVerdict=If[growthLane["laneScore"]<thr,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS","PASS_LOCKED"];
proceduralViolation=Or[
!repairedFailLanesFlagged,
viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
viol["PublicValueAltered"],
viol["TargetRemoved"],
viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
viol["PublicProofClaimed"]
];

outputLockStatus=If[proceduralViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",residualAuditVerdict];

markers=<|
"step21FailCarryMarker"->If[step21W["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",1,0],
"growthFailPreservationMarker"->If[growthLane["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"&&growthLane["laneScore"]<thr,1,0],
"driverIsolationMarker"->If[Length[failDrivers]==2,1,0],
"statusLogicRepairMarker"->If[repairedFailLanesFlagged&&StringQ[outputLockStatus],1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[!viol["RFCOutputAltered"]&&!viol["PublicValueAltered"]&&!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],1,0],
"noFitRetuneMarker"->If[!viol["CurveFitPerformed"]&&!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"]&&!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"]&&!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],1,0],
"noCherryPickMarker"->If[!viol["TargetRemoved"]&&!viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"]&&!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"failedLaneAnalysisScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"growthLaneResidualScoreCarried"->growthLane["laneScore"],
"statusRepairDisciplineScore"->markers["statusLogicRepairMarker"],
"noMutationDisciplineScore"->markers["noMutationMarker"],
"noFitRetuneDisciplineScore"->markers["noFitRetuneMarker"],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->markers["claimBoundaryMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step21W["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP21WCarriedAsFail"->(step21W["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"GrowthLaneBelowThreshold"->(growthLane["laneScore"]<thr),
"GrowthLaneStillFail"->(growthLane["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"FailDriversIdentified"->(Length[failDrivers]==2),
"FailDriversAreS8WeakLensing"->AllTrue[Lookup[failDrivers,"name"],StringContainsQ[#,"S8_proxy_vs"]&],
"PlanckSigma8NotDriver"->(SelectFirst[growthLane["components"],#["name"]=="sigma8_proxy_vs_Planck"&]["score"]>=thr),
"InternalGrowthReadinessNotDriver"->(SelectFirst[growthLane["components"],#["name"]=="growth_bridge_readiness_score"&]["score"]>=thr),
"PendingLanesRemainPending"->(Length[pendingLanes]==4),
"FailLanesFlaggedLogicRepaired"->repairedFailLanesFlagged,
"OutputLockStatusResolvedString"->StringQ[outputLockStatus],
"OutputLockStatusIsFail"->(outputLockStatus=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->!viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
"NoPublicValueAltered"->!viol["PublicValueAltered"],
"NoTargetRemoved"->!viol["TargetRemoved"],
"NoSourceAveragedToImproveFit"->!viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
"NoParameterSearch"->!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],
"NoCurveFit"->!viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
"NoUpstreamPacketAltered"->!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],
"NoFailureSoftening"->!viol["FailureSoftened"],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"FailedLaneAnalysisAboveThreshold"->(scores["failedLaneAnalysisScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

compactCheckScore=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
step21AStatus=If[proceduralViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[compactCheckScore>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

driverLines=Map["- "<>#["name"]<>" score="<>num[#["score"]]<>" fracResidual="<>num[#["fracResidual"]]&,failDrivers];
componentLines=Map["- "<>#["name"]<>" score="<>num[#["score"]]<>" fracResidual="<>num[#["fracResidual"]]<>" driver="<>yn[#["driver"]]&,growthLane["components"]];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 21A COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP21W residual audit","CARRIED_AS_FAIL"],
ln["GrowthMatter_Internal failed lane","CARRIED_AS_FAIL"],
ln["RFC outputs altered","NO"],
ln["Public values altered","NO"],
ln["Targets removed","NO"],
ln["Sources averaged to improve fit","NO"],
ln["Retune / fit / post-hoc correction","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"FAILED LANE ANALYSIS",
ln["failedLane","GrowthMatter_Internal"],
ln["laneScore",num[growthLane["laneScore"]]],
ln["threshold",num[thr]],
ln["laneStatus",growthLane["status"]],
ln["analysisVerdict",analysisVerdict],
ln["growthFailCause",growthFailCause],
"",
"FAIL DRIVERS"
},
driverLines,
{
"",
"GROWTH COMPONENTS"
},
componentLines,
{
"",
"STATUS LOGIC REPAIR"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[TrueQ[#2]||False===#2,yn[#2],#2]]&,logicRepair],
{
ln["repairedFailLanesFlagged",yn[repairedFailLanesFlagged]],
ln["residualAuditVerdict",residualAuditVerdict],
ln["outputLockStatus",outputLockStatus],
"",
"STEP 21A MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 21A SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[compactCheckScore]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic procedural violation detected",yn[proceduralViolation]],
ln["STEP21A outputLockStatus",step21AStatus],
ln["STEP21W residualAuditVerdict remains",residualAuditVerdict],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[step21AStatus=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 21B corrected residual certificate replay or STEP 22W fail-boundary interpretation only","Analyze STEP21A defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not tune, fit, retune, alter RFC outputs, remove targets, average sources to improve fit, soften failure, or claim validation"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 21A analyzes the failed GrowthMatter residual lane and repairs output-status logic only. It preserves STEP 21W as FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS. It changes no RFC values, changes no public values, removes no targets, averages no sources, tunes nothing, fits nothing, retunes nothing, softens no failure, and claims no validation.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",step21AStatus]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

"ExpansionDistance_Internal"-><|"laneScore"->0.954735,"status"->"PASS","components"->3|>,
"GrowthMatter_Internal"-><|"laneScore"->0.939356,"status"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS","components"->5|>,
"W4_ElementResolvedAbundance"-><|"laneScore"->0.994614,"status"->"PASS","components"->2|>
|>;

pending=<|
"Recombination_CMB_Internal"->"readiness markers are not direct public CMB parameters; no forced residual",
"TransferBridge_Internal"->"transfer bridge has no direct numeric transfer-function target in this compact table",
"HaloGalaxy_Internal"->"halo/galaxy readiness markers are not direct cluster S8 observables; no invented mapping",
"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis"->"grouped public abundance-shape residual requires frozen grouping table first"
|>;

growthDrivers=<|
"S8_proxy_vs_DESY3_cosmic_shear"-><|"score"->0.939356,"fracResidual"->0.060644|>,
"S8_proxy_vs_KiDS1000"-><|"score"->0.939356,"fracResidual"->0.060644|>
|>;

viol=<|
"residualValueChanged"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"PublicValueAltered"->False,
"TargetRemoved"->False,
"SourceAveragedToImproveFit"->False,
"ParameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"CurveFitPerformed"->False,
"PostHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"UpstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"UpstreamPacketAltered"->False,
"FailureSoftened"->False,
"ExternalValidationClaimed"->False,
"PublicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

scoredLaneScores=Lookup[Values[lanes],"laneScore"];
failLanes=Select[lanes,#["laneScore"]<thr&];
passLanes=Select[lanes,#["laneScore"]>=thr&];

residualAuditScore=N[Min[scoredLaneScores]];
residualAuditVerdict=If[Length[failLanes]>0,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS","PASS_LOCKED"];

failLanesFlagged=Length[failLanes]==1&&AllTrue[Values[failLanes],#["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"&];
passLanesFlagged=Length[passLanes]==4&&AllTrue[Values[passLanes],#["status"]=="PASS"&];

proceduralViolation=Or[
!failLanesFlagged,
!passLanesFlagged,
residualAuditScore!=step21WOriginal["residualAuditScore"],
viol["residualValueChanged"],
viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
viol["PublicValueAltered"],
viol["TargetRemoved"],
viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
viol["FailureSoftened"],
viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
viol["PublicProofClaimed"]
];

correctedStep21WOutputLockStatus=If[proceduralViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",residualAuditVerdict];

markers=<|
"step21ACarryMarker"->If[step21A["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED",1,0],
"originalFailCarryMarker"->If[step21WOriginal["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",1,0],
"residualScorePreservationMarker"->If[residualAuditScore==step21WOriginal["residualAuditScore"],1,0],
"failLanePreservationMarker"->If[failLanesFlagged,1,0],
"passLanePreservationMarker"->If[passLanesFlagged,1,0],
"pendingLanePreservationMarker"->If[Length[pending]==step21WOriginal["pendingLaneCount"],1,0],
"statusLogicRepairMarker"->If[StringQ[correctedStep21WOutputLockStatus]&&correctedStep21WOutputLockStatus=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",1,0],
"growthDriverPreservationMarker"->If[Length[growthDrivers]==2,1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[!viol["RFCOutputAltered"]&&!viol["PublicValueAltered"]&&!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],1,0],
"noFitRetuneMarker"->If[!viol["CurveFitPerformed"]&&!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"]&&!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"]&&!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"]&&!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"correctedReplayDisciplineScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"correctedResidualAuditScore"->residualAuditScore,
"protocolExecutionDisciplineScore"->step21WOriginal["protocolExecutionDisciplineScore"],
"scoredLaneCount"->Length[lanes],
"passLaneCount"->Length[passLanes],
"failLaneCount"->Length[failLanes],
"pendingLaneCount"->Length[pending],
"stateContinuityScore"->step21A["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP21ALocked"->(step21A["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"OriginalSTEP21WCarriedAsFail"->(step21WOriginal["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"OriginalResidualResultValid"->step21WOriginal["residualResultValid"],
"OriginalStatusLogicDefectAcknowledged"->step21WOriginal["statusLogicDefective"],
"ResidualAuditScorePreserved"->(residualAuditScore==step21WOriginal["residualAuditScore"]),
"GrowthLaneStillBelowThreshold"->(lanes["GrowthMatter_Internal","laneScore"]<thr),
"GrowthLaneStillFail"->(lanes["GrowthMatter_Internal","status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"FailLanesFlaggedRepaired"->failLanesFlagged,
"PassLanesFlagged"->passLanesFlagged,
"PendingLanesPreserved"->(Length[pending]==4),
"CorrectedStep21WStatusResolved"->StringQ[correctedStep21WOutputLockStatus],
"CorrectedStep21WStatusIsFail"->(correctedStep21WOutputLockStatus=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"NoResidualValueChanged"->!viol["residualValueChanged"],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->!viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
"NoPublicValueAltered"->!viol["PublicValueAltered"],
"NoTargetRemoved"->!viol["TargetRemoved"],
"NoSourceAveragedToImproveFit"->!viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
"NoParameterSearch"->!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],
"NoCurveFit"->!viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
"NoUpstreamPacketAltered"->!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],
"NoFailureSoftening"->!viol["FailureSoftened"],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"CorrectedReplayAboveThreshold"->(scores["correctedReplayDisciplineScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(markers["claimBoundaryMarker"]>=thr)
|>;

compactCheckScore=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
step21BStatus=If[proceduralViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[compactCheckScore>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

laneLines=KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},ln[k,"laneScore="<>num[v["laneScore"]]<>" | status="<>v["status"]<>" | components="<>ToString[v["components"]]]],lanes];
pendingLines=KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" reason="<>v],pending];
driverLines=KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" score="<>num[v["score"]]<>" fracResidual="<>num[v["fracResidual"]]],growthDrivers];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 21B COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP21A diagnostic repair","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP21W original residual audit","CARRIED_AS_FAIL"],
ln["Residual values changed","NO"],
ln["RFC outputs altered","NO"],
ln["Public values altered","NO"],
ln["Failure softened","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"CORRECTED STEP 21W REPLAY SUMMARY",
ln["originalSTEP21WStatus",step21WOriginal["status"]],
ln["originalDefect",step21WOriginal["defect"]],
ln["correctedResidualAuditScore",num[residualAuditScore]],
ln["correctedResidualAuditVerdict",residualAuditVerdict],
ln["correctedSTEP21WOutputLockStatus",correctedStep21WOutputLockStatus],
"",
"LANE REPLAY"
},
laneLines,
{
"",
"PENDING LANES PRESERVED"
},
pendingLines,
{
"",
"GROWTH FAIL DRIVERS PRESERVED"
},
driverLines,
{
"",
"STEP 21B MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 21B SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[compactCheckScore]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic procedural violation detected",yn[proceduralViolation]],
ln["STEP21B outputLockStatus",step21BStatus],
ln["Corrected STEP21W residual status remains",correctedStep21WOutputLockStatus],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[step21BStatus=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 22W fail-boundary interpretation only","Analyze STEP21B replay defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not tune, fit, retune, alter RFC outputs, remove targets, average sources to improve fit, soften failure, or claim validation"],
"",
"CLAIM BOUNDARY",
"STEP 21B replays the STEP 21W residual certificate with repaired status logic only. It preserves GrowthMatter_Internal as FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS, changes no residual values, changes no RFC outputs, changes no public values, removes no targets, averages no sources, tunes nothing, fits nothing, retunes nothing, softens no failure, and claims no validation.",
"",
"FINAL STATUS",
ln["LOCKED AS",step21BStatus]
}
];

Print[StringRiffle[out,"\n"]];

ClearAll["Global`*"];

run="STEP 22W"; mod="Fail-Boundary Interpretation Only"; ver="RFC-SU-CLEAN-STEP22W-v1.0-COMPACT"; thr=.95;

step21B=<|
"status"->"PASS_LOCKED",
"correctedReplayDisciplineScore"->1.000000,
"correctedResidualAuditScore"->0.939356,
"correctedResidualAuditVerdict"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
"stateContinuityScore"->0.995588
|>;

step21W=<|
"status"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",
"failedLane"->"GrowthMatter_Internal",
"residualAuditScore"->0.939356,
"failDrivers"->{"S8_proxy_vs_DESY3_cosmic_shear","S8_proxy_vs_KiDS1000"},
"RFC_S8_proxy"->0.808000,
"weakLensing_S8_reference"->0.759000,
"componentScore"->0.939356,
"fracResidual"->0.060644
|>;

passedLanes=<|
"PreBBNParticleMassChain"->0.959049,
"BBN_W2R"->0.991990,
"ExpansionDistance_Internal"->0.954735,
"W4_ElementResolvedAbundance"->0.994614
|>;

pendingLanes=<|
"Recombination_CMB_Internal"->"readiness markers are not direct public CMB parameters",
"TransferBridge_Internal"->"no direct compact transfer-function target",
"HaloGalaxy_Internal"->"readiness markers are not direct cluster S8 observables",
"W3_GroupedNucleosynthesis"->"requires frozen grouping table first"
|>;

interpretation=<|
"PrimaryFailClass"->"LOCALIZED_S8_WEAK_LENSING_TENSION",
"WholeChainFailure"->False,
"GrowthLaneFailure"->True,
"PlanckSigma8Driver"->False,
"InternalGrowthReadinessDriver"->False,
"WeakLensingS8Driver"->True,
"ObservableMappingBoundaryPresent"->True,
"RetuneAuthorized"->False,
"TargetRemovalAuthorized"->False,
"SourceAveragingAuthorized"->False,
"ValidationClaimAuthorized"->False
|>;

boundaryFindings=<|
"Finding1"->"The failure is localized to GrowthMatter_Internal.",
"Finding2"->"The failing components are weak-lensing S8 slots, not Planck sigma8.",
"Finding3"->"RFC-SU S8 proxy remains frozen at 0.808000.",
"Finding4"->"The read-only weak-lensing S8 slots are 0.759000.",
"Finding5"->"The 0.939356 score is below the 0.950000 audit threshold.",
"Finding6"->"This creates an analysis requirement, not permission to tune.",
"Finding7"->"Pending non-comparable lanes remain pending and cannot be forced into residuals."
|>;

allowedInterpretations=<|
"KnownStyleS8TensionReflection"->"allowed_as_interpretation",
"ObservableMappingBoundary"->"allowed_as_interpretation",
"PotentialRFCGrowthWeakness"->"allowed_as_open_question",
"NeedForGrowthLaneDefinitionAudit"->"allowed_as_next_diagnostic",
"ExternalValidationFailure"->"not_claimed",
"ExternalValidationSuccess"->"not_claimed"
|>;

viol=<|
"NewResidualsComputed"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"PublicValueAltered"->False,
"TargetRemoved"->False,
"SourceAveragedToImproveFit"->False,
"ParameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"CurveFitPerformed"->False,
"PostHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"UpstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"UpstreamPacketAltered"->False,
"FailureSoftened"->False,
"FailedLanePromotedToPass"->False,
"ExternalValidationClaimed"->False,
"PublicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

markers=<|
"step21BCarryMarker"->If[step21B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED",1,0],
"step21WFailCarryMarker"->If[step21W["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",1,0],
"growthFailurePreservationMarker"->If[step21B["correctedResidualAuditVerdict"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"&&step21W["componentScore"]<thr,1,0],
"driverInterpretationMarker"->If[interpretation["WeakLensingS8Driver"]&&!interpretation["PlanckSigma8Driver"],1,0],
"boundarySeparationMarker"->If[interpretation["ObservableMappingBoundaryPresent"]&&Length[pendingLanes]==4,1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[!viol["RFCOutputAltered"]&&!viol["PublicValueAltered"]&&!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],1,0],
"noFitRetuneMarker"->If[!viol["CurveFitPerformed"]&&!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"]&&!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"]&&!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],1,0],
"noCherryPickMarker"->If[!viol["TargetRemoved"]&&!viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],1,0],
"failureNotSoftenedMarker"->If[!viol["FailureSoftened"]&&!viol["FailedLanePromotedToPass"],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"]&&!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"failBoundaryInterpretationScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"carriedResidualAuditScore"->step21B["correctedResidualAuditScore"],
"passedLaneCount"->Length[passedLanes],
"failedLaneCount"->1,
"pendingLaneCount"->Length[pendingLanes],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->markers["claimBoundaryMarker"],
"noRetuneDisciplineScore"->markers["noFitRetuneMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step21B["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP21BLocked"->(step21B["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP21WCarriedAsFail"->(step21W["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"ResidualAuditVerdictStillFail"->(step21B["correctedResidualAuditVerdict"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"GrowthLaneStillBelowThreshold"->(step21W["componentScore"]<thr),
"FailedLaneIdentified"->(step21W["failedLane"]=="GrowthMatter_Internal"),
"WeakLensingDriversIdentified"->(Length[step21W["failDrivers"]]==2),
"WholeChainFailureNotClaimed"->!interpretation["WholeChainFailure"],
"PlanckSigma8NotDriver"->!interpretation["PlanckSigma8Driver"],
"WeakLensingS8DriverPreserved"->interpretation["WeakLensingS8Driver"],
"ObservableMappingBoundaryPreserved"->interpretation["ObservableMappingBoundaryPresent"],
"PendingLanesRemainPending"->(Length[pendingLanes]==4),
"NoNewResidualsComputed"->!viol["NewResidualsComputed"],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->!viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
"NoPublicValueAltered"->!viol["PublicValueAltered"],
"NoTargetRemoved"->!viol["TargetRemoved"],
"NoSourceAveragedToImproveFit"->!viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
"NoParameterSearch"->!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],
"NoCurveFit"->!viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
"NoUpstreamPacketAltered"->!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],
"NoFailureSoftening"->!viol["FailureSoftened"],
"NoFailedLanePromotedToPass"->!viol["FailedLanePromotedToPass"],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"FailBoundaryInterpretationAboveThreshold"->(scores["failBoundaryInterpretationScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

proceduralViolation=Or[
!checks["STEP21BLocked"],
!checks["STEP21WCarriedAsFail"],
!checks["ResidualAuditVerdictStillFail"],
!checks["NoFailureSoftening"],
viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
viol["PublicValueAltered"],
viol["TargetRemoved"],
viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
viol["FailedLanePromotedToPass"],
viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
viol["PublicProofClaimed"]
];

compactCheckScore=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[proceduralViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[compactCheckScore>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

passedLines=KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" score="<>num[v]],passedLanes];
pendingLines=KeyValueMap[Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" reason="<>v],pendingLanes];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 22W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP21B corrected replay","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP21W residual fail","CARRIED_AS_FAIL"],
ln["New residuals computed","NO"],
ln["RFC outputs altered","NO"],
ln["Public values altered","NO"],
ln["Retune / fit / post-hoc correction","NO"],
ln["Failure softened","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"FAIL-BOUNDARY INTERPRETATION",
ln["primaryFailClass",interpretation["PrimaryFailClass"]],
ln["failedLane",step21W["failedLane"]],
ln["laneScore",num[step21W["componentScore"]]],
ln["threshold",num[thr]],
ln["RFC_S8_proxy",num[step21W["RFC_S8_proxy"]]],
ln["weakLensing_S8_reference",num[step21W["weakLensing_S8_reference"]]],
ln["fracResidual",num[step21W["fracResidual"]]],
ln["residualVerdict",step21B["correctedResidualAuditVerdict"]],
"",
"PASSED SCORED LANES"
},
passedLines,
{
"",
"PENDING NON-COMPARABLE LANES"
},
pendingLines,
{
"",
"BOUNDARY FINDINGS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,#2]&,boundaryFindings],
{
"",
"ALLOWED INTERPRETATIONS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,#2]&,allowedInterpretations],
{
"",
"INTERPRETATION FLAGS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[TrueQ[#2]||False===#2,yn[#2],#2]]&,interpretation],
{
"",
"STEP 22W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 22W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[NumericQ[#2],num[#2],#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[compactCheckScore]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic procedural violation detected",yn[proceduralViolation]],
ln["STEP22W outputLockStatus",status],

"failDrivers"->{"S8_proxy_vs_DESY3_cosmic_shear","S8_proxy_vs_KiDS1000"}
|>;

growthComponents=<|
"Planck_sigma8"-><|"rfcProxy"->0.808000,"publicSlot"->0.811000,"priorScore"->0.996301,"priorStatus"->"PASS","observableClass"->"CMB_inferred_sigma8","mappingClass"->"direct_growth_amplitude_proxy","driver"->False|>,
"DESY3_3x2pt_S8"-><|"rfcProxy"->0.808000,"publicSlot"->0.776000,"priorScore"->0.960396,"priorStatus"->"PASS","observableClass"->"combined_lensing_clustering_S8","mappingClass"->"composite_growth_proxy","driver"->False|>,
"DESY3_cosmic_shear_S8"-><|"rfcProxy"->0.808000,"publicSlot"->0.759000,"priorScore"->0.939356,"priorStatus"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS","observableClass"->"weak_lensing_only_S8","mappingClass"->"survey_shear_specific_proxy_missing","driver"->True|>,
"KiDS1000_S8"-><|"rfcProxy"->0.808000,"publicSlot"->0.759000,"priorScore"->0.939356,"priorStatus"->"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS","observableClass"->"weak_lensing_only_S8","mappingClass"->"survey_shear_specific_proxy_missing","driver"->True|>,
"Internal_growth_readiness"-><|"rfcProxy"->0.993175,"publicSlot"->"audit_slot","priorScore"->0.993175,"priorStatus"->"PASS","observableClass"->"internal_readiness_score","mappingClass"->"not_public_observable","driver"->False|>
|>;

mappingAudit=<|
"SingleRFCGrowthProxyUsed"->True,
"WeakLensingSpecificForwardModelPresent"->False,
"SurveyCovarianceModelPresent"->False,
"NonlinearShearKernelPresent"->False,
"ObservableSpecificS8MapPresent"->False,
"PlanckLikeGrowthAmplitudePasses"->True,
"Combined3x2ptLanePasses"->True,
"WeakLensingOnlySlotsFail"->True,
"FailureLocalizedToObservableClass"->True
|>;

classification=<|
"PrimaryClassification"->"OBSERVABLE_MAPPING_BOUNDARY_PLUS_S8_TENSION_REFLECTION",
"SecondaryClassification"->"POTENTIAL_GROWTH_LANE_REFINEMENT_NEEDED",
"ConfirmedRFCGlobalFailure"->False,
"ConfirmedRFCGrowthPhysicsFailure"->False,
"ConfirmedObservableMappingGap"->True,
"KnownStyleS8TensionReflected"->True,
"RequiresForwardModelBeforeStrongerClaim"->True,
"RetuneAuthorized"->False,
"TargetRemovalAuthorized"->False,
"SourceAveragingAuthorized"->False,
"ValidationClaimAuthorized"->False
|>;

auditFindings=<|
"Finding1"->"GrowthMatter_Internal fails only against weak-lensing-only S8 slots.",
"Finding2"->"Planck sigma8 and DESY3 3x2pt remain above threshold.",
"Finding3"->"The RFC growth proxy is a frozen single internal amplitude proxy.",
"Finding4"->"A weak-lensing-only observable requires an observable-specific shear/lensing forward map before stronger physical interpretation.",
"Finding5"->"The failure is real and remains FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS.",
"Finding6"->"The failure may reflect known-style S8 tension, mapping incompleteness, or an RFC growth-lane weakness.",
"Finding7"->"No target may be removed, averaged, retuned, or softened."
|>;

nextDiagnosticOptions=<|
"OptionA"->"Freeze a growth-observable hierarchy: sigma8, S8 3x2pt, weak-lensing-only S8, cluster S8.",
"OptionB"->"Build a no-retune weak-lensing forward-map protocol from frozen RFC outputs.",
"OptionC"->"Keep GrowthMatter_Internal failed until observable-specific map exists.",
"OptionD"->"Do not re-score the failed lane without a predeclared mapping protocol."
|>;

viol=<|
"NewResidualsComputed"->False,
"ResidualValuesChanged"->False,
"RFCOutputAltered"->False,
"PublicValueAltered"->False,
"TargetRemoved"->False,
"SourceAveragedToImproveFit"->False,
"ParameterSearchPerformed"->False,
"CurveFitPerformed"->False,
"PostHocCorrectionPerformed"->False,
"UpstreamRetunePerformed"->False,
"UpstreamPacketAltered"->False,
"FailureSoftened"->False,
"FailedLanePromotedToPass"->False,
"ExternalValidationClaimed"->False,
"PublicProofClaimed"->False
|>;

failDrivers=Select[growthComponents,#["driver"]===True&];
passComponents=Select[growthComponents,#["priorStatus"]=="PASS"&];

markers=<|
"step22CarryMarker"->If[step22["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED",1,0],
"step21FailureCarryMarker"->If[step21W["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"&&step21W["laneScore"]<thr,1,0],
"driverClassIsolationMarker"->If[Length[failDrivers]==2&&AllTrue[Lookup[Values[failDrivers],"observableClass"],#=="weak_lensing_only_S8"&],1,0],
"passClassSeparationMarker"->If[Length[passComponents]==3,1,0],
"mappingGapIdentificationMarker"->If[classification["ConfirmedObservableMappingGap"],1,0],
"failurePreservationMarker"->If[!viol["FailureSoftened"]&&!viol["FailedLanePromotedToPass"],1,0],
"noNewResidualMarker"->If[!viol["NewResidualsComputed"]&&!viol["ResidualValuesChanged"],1,0],
"noMutationMarker"->If[!viol["RFCOutputAltered"]&&!viol["PublicValueAltered"]&&!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],1,0],
"noFitRetuneMarker"->If[!viol["CurveFitPerformed"]&&!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"]&&!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"]&&!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],1,0],
"noCherryPickMarker"->If[!viol["TargetRemoved"]&&!viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],1,0],
"claimBoundaryMarker"->If[!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"]&&!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],1,0]
|>;

scores=<|
"growthObservableMappingAuditScore"->N[Mean[Values[markers]]],
"carriedGrowthResidualScore"->step21W["laneScore"],
"failedDriverCount"->Length[failDrivers],
"passingGrowthComponentCount"->Length[passComponents],
"mappingGapDisciplineScore"->markers["mappingGapIdentificationMarker"],
"failurePreservationScore"->markers["failurePreservationMarker"],
"claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"->markers["claimBoundaryMarker"],
"stateContinuityScore"->step22["stateContinuityScore"]
|>;

checks=<|
"STEP22Locked"->(step22["status"]=="PASS_LOCKED"),
"STEP21WCarriedAsFail"->(step21W["status"]=="FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"),
"GrowthLaneStillBelowThreshold"->(step21W["laneScore"]<thr),
"WeakLensingFailDriversPreserved"->(Length[failDrivers]==2),
"FailDriversWeakLensingOnly"->AllTrue[Lookup[Values[failDrivers],"observableClass"],#=="weak_lensing_only_S8"&],
"PlanckSigma8StillPass"->(growthComponents["Planck_sigma8","priorScore"]>=thr),
"DESY3_3x2ptStillPass"->(growthComponents["DESY3_3x2pt_S8","priorScore"]>=thr),
"InternalGrowthReadinessStillPass"->(growthComponents["Internal_growth_readiness","priorScore"]>=thr),
"MappingGapIdentified"->classification["ConfirmedObservableMappingGap"],
"GlobalFailureNotClaimed"->!classification["ConfirmedRFCGlobalFailure"],
"GrowthPhysicsFailureNotConfirmed"->!classification["ConfirmedRFCGrowthPhysicsFailure"],
"RetuneNotAuthorized"->!classification["RetuneAuthorized"],
"TargetRemovalNotAuthorized"->!classification["TargetRemovalAuthorized"],
"SourceAveragingNotAuthorized"->!classification["SourceAveragingAuthorized"],
"ValidationClaimNotAuthorized"->!classification["ValidationClaimAuthorized"],
"NoNewResidualsComputed"->!viol["NewResidualsComputed"],
"NoResidualValuesChanged"->!viol["ResidualValuesChanged"],
"NoRFCOutputAltered"->!viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
"NoPublicValueAltered"->!viol["PublicValueAltered"],
"NoTargetRemoved"->!viol["TargetRemoved"],
"NoSourceAveragedToImproveFit"->!viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
"NoParameterSearch"->!viol["ParameterSearchPerformed"],
"NoCurveFit"->!viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
"NoPostHocCorrection"->!viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
"NoUpstreamRetune"->!viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
"NoUpstreamPacketAltered"->!viol["UpstreamPacketAltered"],
"NoFailureSoftening"->!viol["FailureSoftened"],
"NoFailedLanePromotedToPass"->!viol["FailedLanePromotedToPass"],
"NoExternalValidationClaimed"->!viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
"NoPublicProofClaimed"->!viol["PublicProofClaimed"],
"MarkersNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[markers]),
"ScoresNumeric"->And@@(NumericQ/@Values[scores]),
"MarkersNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[markers],_Missing],
"ScoresNoMissing"->FreeQ[Values[scores],_Missing],
"MappingAuditAboveThreshold"->(scores["growthObservableMappingAuditScore"]>=thr),
"ClaimBoundaryAboveThreshold"->(scores["claimBoundaryDisciplineScore"]>=thr)
|>;

proceduralViolation=Or[
!checks["STEP22Locked"],
!checks["STEP21WCarriedAsFail"],
!checks["GrowthLaneStillBelowThreshold"],
!checks["NoFailureSoftening"],
viol["NewResidualsComputed"],
viol["ResidualValuesChanged"],
viol["RFCOutputAltered"],
viol["PublicValueAltered"],
viol["TargetRemoved"],
viol["SourceAveragedToImproveFit"],
viol["CurveFitPerformed"],
viol["PostHocCorrectionPerformed"],
viol["UpstreamRetunePerformed"],
viol["FailedLanePromotedToPass"],
viol["ExternalValidationClaimed"],
viol["PublicProofClaimed"]
];

compactCheckScore=N[Count[Values[checks],True]/Length[checks]];
status=If[proceduralViolation,"FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS",If[compactCheckScore>=thr,"PASS_LOCKED","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"]];

yn[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"YES","NO"];
pf[x_]:=If[TrueQ[x],"PASS","FAIL"];
num[x_]:=ToString[NumberForm[N[x],{8,6}]];
ln[k_,v_]:=ToString[k]<>": "<>ToString[v];

componentLines=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" | class="<>v["observableClass"]<>" | mapping="<>v["mappingClass"]<>" | priorScore="<>num[v["priorScore"]]<>" | status="<>v["priorStatus"]<>" | driver="<>yn[v["driver"]]],
growthComponents
];

driverLines=KeyValueMap[
Function[{k,v},"- "<>k<>" priorScore="<>num[v["priorScore"]]<>" observableClass="<>v["observableClass"]],
failDrivers
];

out=Join[
{
"RFC-SU CLEAN STEP 23W COMPACT CERTIFICATE",
"========================================",
ln["RUN",run],
ln["MODULE",mod],
ln["VERSION",ver],
"",
"SOURCE GATE",
ln["STEP22W fail-boundary interpretation","CARRIED"],
ln["STEP21W GrowthMatter failure","CARRIED_AS_FAIL"],
ln["New residuals computed","NO"],
ln["Residual values changed","NO"],
ln["RFC outputs altered","NO"],
ln["Public values altered","NO"],
ln["Retune / fit / post-hoc correction","NO"],
ln["Failure softened","NO"],
ln["Audit verdict","PASS"],
"",
"GROWTH OBSERVABLE COMPONENT MAP"
},
componentLines,
{
"",
"FAIL DRIVERS PRESERVED"
},
driverLines,
{
"",
"CLASSIFICATION"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[TrueQ[#2]||False===#2,yn[#2],#2]]&,classification],
{
"",
"AUDIT FINDINGS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,#2]&,auditFindings],
{
"",
"NEXT DIAGNOSTIC OPTIONS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,#2]&,nextDiagnosticOptions],
{
"",
"STEP 23W MARKERS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,num[#2]]&,markers],
{
"",
"STEP 23W SCORES"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,If[NumericQ[#2],num[#2],#2]]&,scores],
{
"",
"CHECKS"
},
KeyValueMap[ln[#1,pf[#2]]&,checks],
{
"",
"SCORE POLICY",
ln["Pass threshold",num[thr]],
ln["compact check score",num[compactCheckScore]],
"",
"STATUS",
ln["automatic procedural violation detected",yn[proceduralViolation]],
ln["STEP23W outputLockStatus",status],
ln["GrowthMatter residual verdict remains","FAIL_REQUIRES_ANALYSIS"],
ln["allowedNextUse",If[status=="PASS_LOCKED","Initialize STEP 24W Growth Observable Hierarchy Freeze","Analyze STEP23W mapping-audit defect"]],
ln["forbiddenNextUse","Do not tune, fit, retune, alter RFC outputs, remove targets, average sources to improve fit, soften failure, or claim validation"],
"",

"STEP 23W audits the GrowthMatter observable mapping only. It preserves the GrowthMatter residual failure, computes no new residuals, changes no RFC values, changes no public values, removes no targets, averages no sources, tunes nothing, fits nothing, retunes nothing, softens no failure, and claims no validation.",
"",

}
